## S-DU YING DJÜN. 使徒行传

### Sd. I.

<u><ruby><rb>提阿非罗</rb><rt>DI-O-FI-LO</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>箇本</rb><rt>keh-peng</rt><rb>书</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>教训个</rb><rt>kao-hyüing-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>拣选个</rb><rt>kæn-shün-go</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>升上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>*tʽin*</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>难</rb><rt>næn</rt><rb>以后</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>确实个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-jih-go</rt><rb>凭据</rb><rt>bing-kyü</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活转来个</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>现出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>*teng*</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>神国个</rb><rt>Jing-koh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>聚拢东</rb><rt>jü-long-tong</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等待</rb><rt>teng-dæ</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>*Tʽin*-Vu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>呒多少</rb><rt>m-to-siao</rt><rb>日数</rb><rt>nyih-su</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>圣灵个</rb><rt>Sing-Ling-go</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>聚拢东</rb><rt>jü-long-tong</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>时节</rb><rt>z-tsin</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>挽回</rb><rt>w̆æn-we</rt><rb>国家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>日子</rb><rt>Nyih-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>*Tʽin*-Vu</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>章程</rb><rt>công-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>临着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒玛利亚</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>地个</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>尽头</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>讲好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看东</rb><rt>kʽen-tong</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>升上去</rb><rt>sing-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一朵</rb><rt>ih-tô</rt><rb>云</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>看弗见</rb><rt>kʽen-feh-kyin</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>清盯</rb><rt>tsʽing-ting</rt><rb>看东</rb><rt>kʽen-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>穿勒</rb><rt>cʽün-leh</rt><rb>白</rb><rt>bah</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>旁边</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt></ruby>，<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>朝</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>立勒</rb><rt>lih-leh</rt><rb>看东</rb><rt>kʽen-tong</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>Keh-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>别开</rb><rt>bih-kʽæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>来其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*keh*</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>*sæn*</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>橄榄</rb><rt>Ken-læn</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽô</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>走个</rb><rt>tseo-go</rt><rb>路程</rb><rt>lu-dzing</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>Kyi-jün</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>一间</rb><rt>ih-kæn</rt><rb>楼顶</rb><rt>leo-teng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>庉个</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>安得烈</rb><rt>En-teh-lih</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>多马</rb><rt>To-mô</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>巴多罗买</rb><rt>Pô-to-lo-mæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马太</rb><rt>Mô-tʽa</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb><u>亚勒腓</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-leh-fi-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>锐罗底</rb><rt>Ze-lo-ti</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>个</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>*hyüong-di*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>*kyi-go*</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一心一意</rb><rt>ih-sing-ih-i</rt><rb>弗歇个</rb><rt>feh-hyih-go</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>求求</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>KEH-GO</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>聚拢东</rb><rt>jü-long-tong</rt><rb>门徒个</rb><rt>meng-du-go</rt><rb>名数</rb><rt>ming-su</rt><rb>大约</rb><rt>da-iah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>廿个</rb><rt>nyiæn-go</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>当中央</rb><rt>tông-cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>众位</rb><rt>Cong-we</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大辟</u>个</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>口里</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>预先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>，（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>*Yiu-da*</rt></ruby></u>。）<ruby><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>书</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>自然</rb><rt>z-jün</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>应验个</rb><rt>ing-nyiæn-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>队里</rb><rt>de-li</rt><rb>算进</rb><rt>sön-tsing</rt><rb>在内</rb><rt>dzæ-nen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>职份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>有份</rb><rt>yiu-veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>非义之财</rb><rt>fi-yi-ts-dzæ</rt><rb>买</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>磕倒</rb><rt>kʽeh-tao</rt><rb>地垟里</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt><rb>豁开</rb><rt>hweh-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>肚肠</rb><rt>du-dziang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>流出</rb><rt>liu-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> （<ruby><rb>通</rb><rt>Tʽong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>庉的个</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇丘</rb><rt>keh-kyʽiu</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>土名</rb><rt>tʽu-ming</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚革大马</rb><rt>Üô-keh-da-mô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>血田</rb><rt>Hyüih-din</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>。）<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>圣诗里</rb><rt>Sing-s-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>oh</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>变</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>空地</rb><rt>kʽong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。』 <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>职份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>21, 22</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>来来去去个</rb><rt>læ-læ-kyʽi-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>别开</rb><rt>bih-kʽæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>升上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>聚拢东个</rb><rt>jü-long-tong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其中</rb><rt>gyi-cong</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拣</rb><rt>*kæn*</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>再活个</rb><rt>tsæ-weh-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>举荐</rb><rt>kyü-tsin</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>*ih-go*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>若瑟</rb><rt>Ziah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴撒巴</rb><rt>Pô-sah-pô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹士都</rb><rt>Yiu-z-tu</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>*ih-go*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马提亚</rb><rt>Mô-di-üô</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>拣着个</rb><rt>kæn-djôh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>设立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>办事</rb><rt>bæn-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>使徒个</rb><rt>s-du-go</rt><rb>职份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt><rb>错落</rb><rt>tsʽo-lôh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>职份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>撮</rb><rt>tsʽeh</rt><rb>指末头</rb><rt>ts-meh-den</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撮着</rb><rt>tsʽeh-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马提亚</rb><rt>Mô-di-üô</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>十一个</rb><rt>jih-ih-go</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>算进</rb><rt>sön-tsing</rt><rb>在内</rb><rt>dzæ-nen</rt></ruby>。

---

### Sd. II.

<ruby><rb>五旬节个</rb><rt>WU-SHING-TSIH-GO</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>同心合意</rb><rt>dong-sing-ʽeh-i</rt><rb>做𡍲</rb><rt>tso-tʽah</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>忽然</rb><rt>Hweh-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>猛猛</rb><rt>mang-mang</rt><rb>风</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>吹过</rb><rt>cʽü-ko</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>充满</rb><rt>cʽong-mun</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>坐的个</rb><rt>zo-tih-go</rt><rb>咸间</rb><rt>ʽen-kæn</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>舌头</rb><rt>zih-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>火光</rb><rt>ho-kwông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>岔打岔</rb><rt>tsʽô-tang-tsʽô</rt><rb>分开</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>各人个</rb><rt>kôh-nying-go</rt><rb>头上</rb><rt>deo-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>感动</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讲起</rb><rt>kông-kyʽi</rt><rb>别处</rb><rt>bih-cʽü</rt><rb>土话</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>*Ka*</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>*z-ʽeo*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>虔心</rb><rt>gyin-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>国打国</rb><rt>koh-tang-koh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>聚集拢</rb><rt>jü-jih-long</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>各处</rb><rt>kôh-cʽü</rt><rb>土话</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>懔懔动</rb><rt>ling-ling-dong</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>Tu</rt><rb>吃𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt><rb>稀奇了</rb><rt>hyi-gyi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讲来讲去</rb><rt>kông-læ-kông-kyʽi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>根生土养</rb><rt>keng-sang-tʽu-yiang</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>帕提亚</rb><rt>Pʽô-di-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>米太</rb><rt>Mi-tʽa</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb><u>以拦</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-læn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米所波大米</rb><rt>Mi-sô-po-da-mi</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>加帕多家</rb><rt>Kyüô-pʽô-to-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>本都</rb><rt>Peng-tu</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>亚西亚</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>弗吕家</rb><rt>Feh-li-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>旁非利亚</rb><rt>Bông-fi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>古利奈</rb><rt>Kwu-li-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近个</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>吕彼亚</rb><rt>Li-pe-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>*yiu-sing*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>*yiu-sing*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>入</rb><rt>jih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革哩底</rb><rt>Keh-li-ti</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚拉伯</rb><rt>Üô-lah-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>各处</rb><rt>*kôh-cʽü*</rt><rb>土话</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。」<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>疑惑不决</rb><rt>nyi-ʽôh-peh-kyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>讲究</rb><rt>kông-kyiu</rt></ruby>？」<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>有得个</rb><rt>Yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>取笑</rb><rt>cʽü-siao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>新酒</rb><rt>sing-tsiu</rt><rb>吃醉的了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-tse-tih-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, Keh-go fong-sing ih yiang-kʽæ. 或话，箇个风声一扬开。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, sing-li m-deo-jü. 或话，心里呒头绪。（原注）</sup>

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>十一个</rb><rt>jih-ih-go</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>立起身</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>响响</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

「<u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇样</rb><rt>keh-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>仔细</rb><rt>ts-si</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>猜</rb><rt>tsʽæ</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>吃酒醉</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-tsiu-tse</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>酒醉</rb><rt>tsiu-tse</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>九</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>点钟</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约珥</rb><rt>Iah-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> 『<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>结煞个</rb><rt>Kyih-sah-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>血气</rb><rt>hyüih-kyʽi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>后生</rb><rt>ʽeo-sang</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>老成人</rb><rt>lao-dzing-nying</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>梦</rb><rt>mong</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>丫头</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地下</rb><rt>di-ʽô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>烟</rb><rt>in</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>威严</rb><rt>w̆e-nyin</rt><rb>光明个</rb><rt>kwông-ming-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>之前</rb><rt>ts-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日头</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>变勒</rb><rt>pin-leh</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>heh-en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>月亮</rb><rt>yüih-liang</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>变勒</rb><rt>pin-leh</rt><rb>血红</rb><rt>hyüih-*ʽong*</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>。』 

<sup>22</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>拿撒勒</u>个</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>明当当</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>设立个</rb><rt>shih-lih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>凭据</rb><rt>*bing-kyü*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。）<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>定规</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>预先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>恶手</rb><rt>ôh-siu</rt><rb>驮来</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>钉煞</rb><rt>ting-sah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>*jih-z-kô-zông*</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>解掉</rb><rt>ka-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>苦楚</rb><rt>kwʽu-tsʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>长通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>缚住</rb><rt>bo-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>大辟</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>长通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>存</rb><rt>dzeng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>顺手边</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>移弗动</rb><rt>yi-feh-dong</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>舌头</rb><rt>zih-deo</rt><rb>高兴</rb><rt>kao-hying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>暂寓</rb><rt>dzæn-nyü</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>坟墓里</rb><rt>*veng-mo-li*</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>错落</rb><rt>tsʽo-lôh</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>活灵</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阴司里</rb><rt>ing-s-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>烂糊</rb><rt>læn-wu</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>活命个</rb><rt>Weh-ming-go</rt><rb>路头</rb><rt>lu-deo</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>点</rb><rt>tin</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>依心满足</rb><rt>i-sing-mun-tsoh</rt></ruby>。』 

<sup>29</sup> 「<ruby><rb>众位</rb><rt>Cong-we</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>让</rb><rt>nyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>放胆</rb><rt>fông-tæn</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大辟</u>个</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt><rb>葬好</rb><rt>tsông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>坟墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>罚咒过了</rb><rt>vah-tsiu-ko-liao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>举上</rb><rt>kyü-zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>预先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>再活个</rb><rt>tsæ-weh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>活灵</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>错落</rb><rt>tsʽo-lôh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阴司里</rb><rt>ing-s-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>烂糊</rb><rt>læn-wu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, ngô kʽo-yi. 或话，我可以。（原注）</sup>

<sup>32</sup> 「<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>*Næn-kæn*</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>举上</rb><rt>kyü-zông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>顺手边</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>*Tʽin*-Vu</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>应许个</rb><rt>ing-hyü-go</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>听闻个</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>赐落来</rb><rt>s-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>大辟</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>顺手边</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>踏脚凳</rb><rt>dah-kyiah-teng</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>话起来</rb><rt>wô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>通</rb><rt>tʽong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>确确实实</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kyʽüoh-jih-jih</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>钉煞</rb><rt>ting-sah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>设立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>戳心</rb><rt>cʽoh-sing</rt><rb>难过</rb><rt>næn-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>余多</rb><rt>yü-to</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>众位</rb><rt>Cong-we</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奉</rb><rt>vong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>赦罪个</rb><rt>sô-ze-go</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>远处个</rb><rt>yün-cʽü-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>召拢来个</rb><rt>dziao-long-læ-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>40</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>*Pe-teh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>血心</rb><rt>hyüih-sing</rt><rb>劝劝</rb><rt>*kyʽün-kyʽün*</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>脱出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>横绷勋流</rb><rt>w̆ang-pang-hyüing-liu</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>加凑个</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo-go</rt><rb>数目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>大约</rb><rt>da-iah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>三千</rb><rt>sæn-tsʽin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>牢牢</rb><rt>lao-lao</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>使徒个</rb><rt>s-du-go</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>时常</rb><rt>z-djông</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>相通</rb><rt>siang-tʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𤖼</rb><rt>pʽah</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>怕起来</rb><rt>pʽô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>聚带拢</rb><rt>jü-tæn-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>打通</rb><rt>tang-tʽong</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>卖掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>货物</rb><rt>ho-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>各人个</rb><rt>kôh-nying-go</rt><rb>用场</rb><rt>yüong-dziang</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>每日</rb><rt>Me-nyih</rt><rb>同心合意</rb><rt>dong-sing-ʽeh-i</rt><rb>不常</rb><rt>peh-djông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>𤖼</rb><rt>pʽah</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>欢欢喜喜</rb><rt>hwun-hwun-hyi-hyi</rt><rb>真心</rb><rt>tsing-sing</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>饭</rb><rt>væn</rt></ruby>，<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>赞美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>得救个</rb><rt>teh-kyiu-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>加凑</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby>。

### Sd. III.

<ruby><rb>下半日</rb><rt>ʽÔ-PUN-NYIH</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>点钟</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>娘胎</rb><rt>nyiang-tʽæ</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拐脚个</rb><rt>kwa-kyiah-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>揹起</rb><rt>pe-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>圣殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>*meng*</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>美</u>门</rb><rt>Me-meng</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>好事</rb><rt>hao-z</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>好事</rb><rt>hao-z</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>清盯</rb><rt>tsʽing-ting</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>liu-sing</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>*kʽen*</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>金子</rb><rt>Kying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>顺只</rb><rt>jing-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>𠡒</rb><rt>te</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>脚底</rb><rt>kyiah-ti</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>脚㬹</rb><rt>kyiah-tsang</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>健争了</rb><rt>gyin-tsang-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>跳起来</rb><rt>tʽiao-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立立</rb><rt>lih-lih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走走</rb><rt>tseo-tseo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走走</rb><rt>tseo-tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跳跳</rb><rt>tʽiao-tʽiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赞美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>赞美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>圣殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>美</u>门口</rb><rt>Me-meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>讨饭</rb><rt>tʽao-væn</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>十分</rb><rt>jih-feng</rt><rb>惊骇</rb><rt>kying-hyiæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>碰着个</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>医好个</rb><rt>i-hao-go</rt><rb>拐脚</rb><rt>kwa-kyiah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>*siu*</rt><rb>拉勒</rb><rt>l̆a-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>奔拢</rb><rt>peng-long</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>所罗门</u>个</rb><rt>Sô-lo-meng-go</rt><rb>廊屋</rb><rt>lông-oh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>箇起</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>清盯</rb><rt>tsʽing-ting</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好像</rb><rt>hao-ziang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>虔心</rb><rt>gyin-sing</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>个</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗个</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>Nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>解上去个</rb><rt>ka-zông-kyʽi-go</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>定规</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>当面前</rb><rt>tông-min-zin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>弃掉个</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>正直个</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反转</rb><rt>*fæn-cün*</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>饶放</rb><rt>nyiao-fông</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>凶手</rb><rt>hyüong-siu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>活命个</rb><rt>weh-ming-go</rt><rb>根源</rb><rt>Keng-nyün</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活转来个</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>*ah-lah*</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>健争起来</rb><rt>gyin-tsang-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>痊愈</rb><rt>djün-yü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, Kyüing-wông. 或话，君王。（原注）</sup>

<sup>17</sup> 「<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>*z-ken*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>先知人个</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>口里</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>预先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>难</rb><rt>næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>应了</rb><rt>ing-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt><rb>弃邪归正</rb><rt>kyʽi-zia-kwe-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>涂掉</rb><rt>du-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>安慰个</rb><rt>en-w̆e-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>预先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>挽回</rb><rt>w̆æn-we</rt><rb>万物</rb><rt>væn-veh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>开辟</rb><rt>kʽæ-bih</rt><rb>以来</rb><rt>yi-læ</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>口里</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话个</rb><rt>wô-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>*ah-lah*</rt><rb>列位</rb><rt>lih-we</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>举上</rb><rt>kyü-zông</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>Sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>Sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒母耳</rb><rt>Sah-meo-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一直落来</rb><rt>ih-dzih-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>预先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>先知人个</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>后裔</rb><rt>ʽeo-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有份</rb><rt>yiu-veng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>设立个</rb><rt>shih-lih-go</rt><rb>约</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>天下</rb><rt>Tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>举上</rb><rt>kyü-zông</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>Nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>罪恶</rb><rt>ze-ôh</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Sd. IV.

<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>圣殿里个</rb><rt>sing-din-li-go</rt><rb>侍卫</rb><rt>z-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒土该</rb><rt>Sah-tʽu-kæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>突然</rb><rt>deh-jün</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>死仔再活个</rb><rt>si-ts-tsæ-weh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>难熬</rb><rt>næn-ngao</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>动手</rb><rt>dong-siu</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>关</rb><rt>kwæn</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>班房里</rb><rt>pæn-vông-li</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>夜了</rb><rt>yia-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>Tʽing-meng</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>相信了</rb><rt>siang-sing-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>男个</rb><rt>nen-go</rt><rb>数目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>约来</rb><rt>iah-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五千</rb><rt>ng-tsʽin</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>DI-NYI</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt></ruby>，<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚拿</rb><rt>Üô-nô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该亚法</rb><rt>Kæ-üô-fah</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>亚力山大</rb><rt>Üô-lih-sæn-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>祭司头个</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo-go</rt><rb>族种</rb><rt>dzoh-cong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>当中央</rb><rt>tông-cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>盘问</rb><rt>bun-meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>靠</rb><rt>kʽao</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>感动</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>，<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>病人</rb><rt>bing-nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>teh-djôh-go</rt><rb>好处</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>盘问</rb><rt>bun-meng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>*kwun-fu*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>钉</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活转来个</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>痊愈</rb><rt>djün-yü</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>起屋个</rb><rt>kyʽi-oh-go</rt><rb>师父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>弃掉个</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao-go</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>*oh*</rt><rb>角落头</rb><rt>kôh-lôh-den</rt><rb>头一块</rb><rt>deo-ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>救星</rb><rt>kyiu-sing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>咸天下</rb><rt>ʽen-tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>赐落来</rb><rt>s-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>侃侃而谈</rb><rt>kʽæn-kʽæn-r-dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>识字个</rb><rt>sih-z-go</rt><rb>粗人</rb><rt>tsʽu-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>稀奇了</rb><rt>hyi-gyi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>认得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>队伙</rb><rt>de-ho</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>医好个</rb><rt>i-hao-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>立东</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>扳驳</rb><rt>pæn-poh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>大堂</rb><rt>do-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>办法</rb><rt>bæn-fah</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt><rb>做过</rb><rt>tso-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>庉东</rb><rt>deng-tong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒得个</rb><rt>m-neh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>箇起</rb><rt>*keh-kyʽi*</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>*z-tʽi*</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>扬开</rb><rt>yiang-kʽæ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>重重</rb><rt>djong-djong</rt><rb>狠</rb><rt>heng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>许</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>警戒</rb><rt>kying-kyiæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>出管</rb><rt>cʽih-kwun</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>提起</rb><rt>di-kyʽi</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>听</rb><rt>Tʽing</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>过于</rb><rt>ko-ü</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>忖忖看</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng-kʽen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>听闻个</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗得弗</rb><rt>feh-teh-feh</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>做过个</rb><rt>tso-ko-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>赞美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>*kwun-fu*</rt><rb>打算弗出</rb><rt>tang-sön-feh-cʽih</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>罚</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>狠</rb><rt>heng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一番</rb><rt>ih-fæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>Pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>箇起</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>医好个</rb><rt>i-hao-go</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>年纪</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四十多</rb><rt>s-jih-to</rt><rb>岁</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*Keh*</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>*liang*</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>*s-du*</rt><rb>放出</rb><rt>fông-cʽih</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>队伙</rb><rt>de-ho</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>同心合意</rb><rt>dong-sing-ʽeh-i</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>万物</rb><rt>væn-veh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大辟</u>个</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>口里</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>别国</rb><rt>bih-koh</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>作吵</rb><rt>tsôh-tsʽao</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>痴心妄想</rb><rt>cʽü-sing-vông-siang</rt></ruby>？<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>世间上个</rb><rt>Shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>君王</rb><rt>kyüing-wông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>立起来</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聚集拢</rb><rt>jü-jih-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>抵敌</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。」 <sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>本丢</rb><rt>Peng-tiu</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>抵敌</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>傅油个</rb><rt>fu-yiu-go</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>Nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>预先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>定规个</rb><rt>ding-kwe-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>29, 30</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>狠</rb><rt>heng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>Nu-boh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做出来</rb><rt>tso-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>胆量</rb><rt>tæn-liang</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] Kyi-toh *go* *ka-shih*, *ziu-z* fu-yiu Cü-kwu. <u>基督</u>个解说，就是傅油主顾。（原注）</sup>

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>祷告好</rb><rt>Tao-kao-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聚拢个</rb><rt>jü-long-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>动起来</rb><rt>dong-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>感动</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放胆</rb><rt>fông-tæn</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>CONG</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>同心合意</rb><rt>dong-sing-ʽeh-i</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>通来通去</rb><rt>tʽong-læ-tʽong-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>活转来个</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>临着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>缺少长短</rb><rt>kyʽüih-siao-dziang-tön</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>居</rb><rt>ke</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt><rb>屋宇</rb><rt>oh-yü</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>卖掉仔</rb><rt>ma-diao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>卖掉个</rb><rt>ma-diao-go</rt><rb>价钿</rb><rt>kô-din</rt></ruby> <sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>使徒个</rb><rt>s-du-go</rt><rb>脚下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>各人个</rb><rt>kôh-nying-go</rt><rb>用场</rb><rt>yüong-dziang</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>居比路</rb><rt>Kyü-pi-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>*ming-z*</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>*kyiao-leh*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约西</rb><rt>Iah-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u>，（<ruby><rb>翻出来</rb><rt>fæn-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>劝化</rb><rt>kyʽün-hwô</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>解说</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt></ruby>。）<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一丘</rb><rt>ih-kyʽiu</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>卖掉仔</rb><rt>ma-diao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>使徒个</rb><rt>s-du-go</rt><rb>脚下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>。
