### Sd. X.

<u><ruby><rb>该撒利亚</rb><rt>KÆ-SAH-LI-ÜÔ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥尼流</rb><rt>Ko-nyi-liu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇营个</rb><rt>keh-ying-go</rt><rb>百总</rb><rt>pah-tsong</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>虔心</rb><rt>gyin-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>和家</rb><rt>ʽo-kô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>怕惧</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>广行</rb><rt>kwông-ʽang</rt><rb>好事</rb><rt>hao-z</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>下半日</rb><rt>ʽÔ-pun-nyih</rt><rb>毛</rb><rt>mao</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>点钟</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>明明</rb><rt>ming-ming</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>显灵</rb><rt>hyin-ling</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>哥尼流</rb><rt>Ko-nyi-liu</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>哥尼流</rb><rt>Ko-nyi-liu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>清盯</rb><rt>tsʽing-ting</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吃一惊</rb><rt>kyʽih-ih-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>*Tʽin-s*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>好事</rb><rt>hao-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>升上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>记得间了</rb><rt>kyi-teh-kæn-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约帕</rb><rt>Iah-pʽô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>耽搁</rb><rt>tæn-kôh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>皮匠</rb><rt>bi-ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 41. 耶（稣）后（头）41（年）。（原注）</sup>

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>哥尼流</rb><rt>Ko-nyi-liu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>佣人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>身边</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt><rb>侍候东</rb><rt>z-ʽeo-tong</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>虔心个</rb><rt>gyin-sing-go</rt><rb>营兵</rb><rt>ying-ping</rt></ruby>，<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约帕</rb><rt>Iah-pʽô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>三个</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>约来</rb><rt>iah-læ</rt><rb>昼过</rb><rt>tsiu-ko</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>光景</rb><rt>kwông-kying</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>屋顶</rb><rt>oh-teng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>*ʽEo-deo*</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>肚饥</rb><rt>du-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>正好</rb><rt>tsing-hao</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>备办</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>忽然</rb><rt>hweh-jün</rt><rb>倦去</rb><rt>gyün-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>开开来</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽæ-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-lôh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>一块</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>布</rb><rt>pu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>缚勒</rb><rt>bo-leh</rt><rb>四只</rb><rt>s-tsah</rt><rb>角</rb><rt>kôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>宕落</rb><rt>dang-lôh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地垟里</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>百样</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>四只脚个</rb><rt>s-tsah-kyiah-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>野兽</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>虫豸</rb><rt>djong-dzi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>天里个</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>鸟</rb><rt>tiao</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>声音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>杀杀</rb><rt>sah-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吃仔</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ts</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使弗得个</rb><rt>s-feh-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>洁净个</rb><rt>kyih-zing-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>吃过</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ko</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>第二遭</rb><rt>Di-nyi-tsao</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>声音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇种介</rb><rt>Keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>三遭</rb><rt>sæn-tsao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>收上</rb><rt>siu-zông</rt><rb>天里</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>正好</rb><rt>tsing-hao</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>疑惑不决</rb><rt>nyi-ʽôh-peh-kyüih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看见个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>显灵是</rb><rt>hyin-ling-z</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥尼流</rb><rt>Ko-nyi-liu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差来个</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西门</u>个</rb><rt>Si-meng-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耽搁</rb><rt>tæn-kôh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>正</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>思忖</rb><rt>s-tsʽeng</rt><rb>显灵个</rb><rt>hyin-ling-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>三个</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>甮</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>疑惑</rb><rt>nyi-ʽôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走落去</rb><rt>tseo-lôh-kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥尼流</rb><rt>Ko-nyi-liu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差来个</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>寻个</rb><rt>zing-go</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>？」<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>百总</rb><rt>Pah-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥尼流</rb><rt>Ko-nyi-liu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>怕惧</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>犹太</u>个</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>称赞个</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>蒙</rb><rt>mong</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>天使个</rb><rt>tʽin-s-go</rt><rb>默示</rb><rt>meh-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>*Pe-teh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>进来</rb><rt>tsing-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>留</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约帕</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-pʽô-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒利亚</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>哥尼流</rb><rt>Ko-nyi-liu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>讴拢</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>亲眷</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyün</rt><rb>自家人</rb><rt>zi-kô-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>知己</rb><rt>cü-kyi</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>等候间</rb><rt>teng-ʽeo-kæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>哥尼流</rb><rt>Ko-nyi-liu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扑倒</rb><rt>pʽoh-tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>脚下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>挡</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>起来</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爬起</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>边</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>边</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>走进去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聚拢间</rb><rt>jü-long-kæn</rt></ruby>，<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>亲近</rb><rt>tsʽing-gying</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>作兴个</rb><rt>tsôh-hying-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>随便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>推辞</rb><rt>tʽe-dz</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>哥尼流</rb><rt>Ko-nyi-liu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>四</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>下半日</rb><rt>ʽô-pun-nyih</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>点钟</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>忽然</rb><rt>hweh-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt><rb>雪刮亮个</rb><rt>shih-kwah-liang-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>31</sup> 『<u><ruby><rb>哥尼流</rb><rt>Ko-nyi-liu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>听闻了</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>好事</rb><rt>hao-z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>记得间了</rb><rt>kyi-teh-kæn-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约帕</rb><rt>Iah-pʽô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>耽搁</rb><rt>tæn-kôh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>皮匠</rb><rt>bi-ziang</rt><rb>师父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。』<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>*Keh-tsao*</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>实惠</rb><rt>jih-we</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>毫无</rb><rt>ʽao-vu</rt><rb>偏曲</rb><rt>pʽin-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>万国</rb><rt>væn-koh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>怕惧</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>做人个</rb><rt>tso-nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>和平个</rb><rt>ʽo-bing-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>。（<ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>*Kyi-toh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>万国人个</rb><rt>væn-koh-nying-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。）<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>*dao-li*</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>传个</rb><rt>djün-go</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做出来个</rb><rt>tso-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>*Ng-lah*</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>*hyiao-teh*</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>*ziang*</rt><rb>傅</rb><rt>fu</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt><rb>封</rb><rt>fong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>拿撒勒</u>个</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>走来走去</rb><rt>tseo-læ-tseo-kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>好事</rb><rt>hao-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>医好</rb><rt>i-hao</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>魔鬼</rb><rt>mo-kwe</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>制服个</rb><rt>cü-voh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> （<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。）<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>挂</rb><rt>kwô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>树上</rb><rt>jü-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>40</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>Keh-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>明明</rb><rt>ming-ming</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>Feh-z</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>预先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>拣选</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>喝个</rb><rt>hah-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>定规</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>活人</rb><rt>weh-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>*yia*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>罪个</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>饶赦</rb><rt>nyiao-sô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>44</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>临着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-liao</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>各处</rb><rt>kôh-cʽü</rt><rb>土话</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>赞美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>47</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>受勒</rb><rt>ziu-leh</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>一样个</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>*keh-tsao*</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>禁住</rb><rt>kying-djü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>留</rb><rt>liu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉上</rb><rt>deng-zông</rt><rb>几</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。
