### Sd. XI.

<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>LÆ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>批评</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>3</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>*z-ken*</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>挨次序</rb><rt>a-tsʽ-jü</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>5</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约帕</rb><rt>Iah-pʽô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>倦去</rb><rt>gyün-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>显灵</rb><rt>hyin-ling</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>一块</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>布</rb><rt>pu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>缚勒</rb><rt>*bo-leh*</rt><rb>四只</rb><rt>s-tsah</rt><rb>角</rb><rt>kôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天里</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>宕落来</rb><rt>dang-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>清盯</rb><rt>tsʽing-ting</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>四只脚个</rb><rt>s-tsah-kyiah-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>野兽</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>虫豸</rb><rt>djong-dzi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>天里个</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>鸟</rb><rt>tiao</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>声音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>杀杀</rb><rt>sah-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吃仔</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ts</rt></ruby>。』<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使弗得个</rb><rt>s-feh-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>洁净个</rb><rt>kyih-zing-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>驮进</rb><rt>do-tsing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>嘴巴里</rb><rt>cü-pô-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。』<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>第二遭</rb><rt>Di-nyi-tsao</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>声音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天里</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>。』<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇种介</rb><rt>Keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>三遭</rb><rt>sæn-tsao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>收上</rb><rt>siu-zông</rt><rb>天里</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>Kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>三个</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来间个</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒利亚</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差来</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>甮</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>疑惑</rb><rt>nyi-ʽôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>荡头</rb><rt>Dông-deo</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>六个</rb><rt>loh-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>箇份</rb><rt>keh-veng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>告诉</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>立东</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约帕</rb><rt>Iah-pʽô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>尔自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>和家</rb><rt>ʽo-kô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得救</rb><rt>teh-kyiu</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。』<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>只勒</rb><rt>*tsih-leh*</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>临着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>起初</rb><rt>kyʽi-tsʽu</rt><rb>临着</rb><rt>*ling-djôh*</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>记得到</rb><rt>kyi-teh-tao</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>：『<u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>圣灵个</rb><rt>Sing-Ling-go</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>恩赐</rb><rt>eng-s</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>阻住</rb><rt>tsu-djü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>呀</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>响</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>赞美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>看起来</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>WE-LEH</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>士提反</rb><rt>Z-di-fæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇起</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>逼难个</rb><rt>pih-næn-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>散开间个</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ-kæn-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓尼基</rb><rt>Fi-nyi-kyi</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>居比路</rb><rt>Kyü-pi-lu</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>内中</rb><rt>Nen-cong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>居比路</rb><rt>Kyü-pi-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>古利奈</rb><rt>Kwu-li-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>亲手</rb><rt>tsʽing-siu</rt><rb>帮助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>归服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb><u>耶路撒冷</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng-go</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>风声</rb><rt>fong-sing</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>走到</rb><rt>Tseo-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>劝</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>坚固个</rb><rt>kyin-kwu-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>亲近</rb><rt>tsʽing-gying</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>好人</rb><rt>hao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>感动个</rb><rt>ken-dong-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>归服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 42. 耶（稣）后（头）42（年）。（原注）</sup>

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大数</rb><rt>Da-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>扫罗</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>碰着仔</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh-ts</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>长</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>聚集拢</rb><rt>jü-jih-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>徒</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-du</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>起头个</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 43. 耶（稣）后（头）43（年）。（原注）</sup>

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>*kyi-go*</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>内中</rb><rt>Nen-cong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚迦布</rb><rt>Üô-kyüô-pu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立起身</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>感动</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>荒年</rb><rt>hwông-nyin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>革老丢</u>个</rb><rt>Keh-lao-tiu-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>有了</rb><rt>yiu-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立定</rb><rt>lih-ding</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>带去</rb><rt>ta-kyʽi</rt><rb>供应</rb><rt>kong-ing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>庉间个</rb><rt>deng-kæn-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>扫罗</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>驮去</rb><rt>do-kyʽi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 44. 耶（稣）后（头）44（年）。（原注）</sup>

---

### Sd. XII.

<ruby><rb>当</rb><rt>TÔNG</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>动手</rb><rt>dong-siu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>教会里</rb><rt>kyiao-we-li</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约翰</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>Kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>。（<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*Keh-go*</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>*z-ʽeo*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>无酵节</rb><rt>Vu-kao-tsih</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>。）<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>抲来</rb><rt>Kʽô-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>挈落</rb><rt>kyʽih-lôh</rt><rb>牢监里</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>十六个</rb><rt>jih-loh-go</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>管守</rb><rt>kwun-siu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-leh</rt><rb>逾越节</rb><rt>Yü-yüih-tsih</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>管守</rb><rt>kwun-siu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>牢监里</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>切心</rb><rt>tsʽih-sing</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, feh-hyih-go. 或话，弗歇个。（原注）</sup>

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打账</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>拖</rb><rt>tʽo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出个</rb><rt>cʽih-go</rt><rb>头夜</rb><rt>deo-yia</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>困</rb><rt>kwʽeng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连住</rb><rt>lin-djü</rt><rb>两根</rb><rt>liang-keng</rt><rb>链条</rb><rt>lin-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>管守</rb><rt>kwun-siu</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>牢门口</rb><rt>lao-meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>守间</rb><rt>siu-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>忽而然</rb><rt>hweh-r-jün</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>透出</rb><rt>tʽeo-cʽih</rt><rb>箇间</rb><rt>keh-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>搭搭</rb><rt>tah-tah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>腰胁</rb><rt>iao-hyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>苏醒</rb><rt>su-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>快快</rb><rt>Kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>手里个</rb><rt>Siu-li-go</rt><rb>链条</rb><rt>lin-diao</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>褪落</rb><rt>tʽeng-lôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>Tʽin-s</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>缴身带</rb><rt>Kyiao-sing-ta</rt><rb>系仔</rb><rt>kyi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鞋带</rb><rt>ʽa-ta</rt><rb>缚仔</rb><rt>bo-ts</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>*Pe-teh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>外头个</rb><rt>nga-deo-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>披仔</rb><rt>pʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>箇间</rb><rt>*keh-kæn*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>忖弗到</rb><rt>tsʽeng-feh-tao</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真个</rb><rt>tsing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>道是</rb><rt>dao-z</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>显灵</rb><rt>hyin-ling</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>第一</rb><rt>di-ih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>第二道</rb><rt>di-nyi-dao</rt><rb>守间</rb><rt>siu-kæn</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇道</rb><rt>keh-dao</rt><rb>通</rb><rt>tʽong</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>铁门</rb><rt>tʽih-meng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>走出仔</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-leh</rt><rb>一埭</rb><rt>ih-da</rt><rb>街</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>忽然</rb><rt>hweh-jün</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>醒头到</rb><rt>sing-deo-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>实惠</rb><rt>jih-we</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>*ko-jün*</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>希律</u>个</rb><rt>Hyi-lih-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>想望个</rb><rt>siang-vông-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>马利亚</u>个</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马可</rb><rt>Mô-kʽo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>敲敲</rb><rt>kʽao-kʽao</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>墙门</rb><rt>ziang-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>使女</rb><rt>s-nyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗大</rb><rt>Lo-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt><rb>听听看</rb><rt>tʽing-tʽing-kʽen</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>听出</rb><rt>Tʽing-cʽih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>彼得</u>个</rb><rt>Pe-teh-go</rt><rb>声音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>欢喜弗煞</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-feh-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>*lin*</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>*tu*</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奔进去</rb><rt>peng-tsing-kyʽi</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>发憨了</rb><rt>fah-hen-de</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>煞扣</rb><rt>sah-kʽeo</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗歇个</rb><rt>feh-hyih-go</rt><rb>敲</rb><rt>kʽao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>*meng*</rt><rb>开开</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>奇怪</rb><rt>gyi-kwa</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>*Pe-teh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>抈抈</rb><rt>yüih-yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>覅</rb><rt>fæ</rt><rb>响</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>牢监</rb><rt>lao-kæn</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>详细</rb><rt>dziang-si</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>别</rb><rt>bih</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, meng-meng-kʽen. 或话，问问看。（原注）</sup>

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>着急</rb><rt>dziah-kyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>寻弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>审问</rb><rt>sing-meng</rt><rb>管守个</rb><rt>kwun-siu-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>拖</rb><rt>tʽo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>*sah-diao*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒利亚</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>耽搁间</rb><rt>tæn-kôh-kæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>推罗</rb><rt>Tʽe-lo</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>西顿</rb><rt>Si-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>斩齐</rb><rt>tsæn-zi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>劝心</rb><rt>kyʽün-sing</rt><rb>王个</rb><rt>wông-go</rt><rb>近身官</rb><rt>gying-sing-kwun</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯拉士都</rb><rt>Pah-lah-z-tu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>求和</rb><rt>gyiu-ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>地方个</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>粮食</rb><rt>liang-zih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>纯</rb><rt>jing</rt><rb>靠</rb><rt>kʽao</rt><rb>王个</rb><rt>wông-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>*di-fông*</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>定规个</rb><rt>ding-kwe-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>穿勒</rb><rt>cʽün-leh</rt><rb>朝衣</rb><rt>dziao-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坐勒</rb><rt>zo-leh</rt><rb>王位</rb><rt>wông-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>响响</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>jing-ming-go</rt><rb>声音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>声音</rb><rt>*sing-ing*</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>虫</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>越发</rb><rt>*yüih-fah*</rt><rb>兴旺</rb><rt>hying-wông</rt><rb>行开去了</rb><rt>ʽang-kʽæ-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>扫罗</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>供应</rb><rt>kong-ing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>做好</rb><rt>tso-hao</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>转来</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马可</rb><rt>Mô-kʽo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, tʽôh-fu gyi. 或话，托付其。（原注）</sup>

---

### Sd. XIII.

<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>LÆ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教会里</rb><rt>kyiao-we-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>教师</rb><rt>kyiao-s</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>西面</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>尼结</rb><rt>Nyi-kyih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>古利奈</rb><rt>Kwu-li-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>路求</rb><rt>Lu-gyiu</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>藩王</rb><rt>væn-wông</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>养大个</rb><rt>iang-do-go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><u><ruby><rb>马念</rb><rt>Mô-nyiæn</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>扫罗</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>分派</rb><rt>feng-pʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>扫罗</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>召</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 45. 耶（稣）后（头）45（年）。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, Hyi-lih-go jü-hyüong. 或话，<u>希律</u>个乳兄。（原注）</sup>

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>差去</rb><rt>tsʽa-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西流基</rb><rt>Si-liu-kyi</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>驶</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>居比路</rb><rt>Kyü-pi-lu</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉米</rb><rt>Sah-lah-mi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>聚会堂里</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>帮手</rb><rt>pông-siu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>穿过</rb><rt>Cʽün-ko</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>海岛</rb><rt>hæ-tao</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>帕弗</rb><rt>Pʽô-feh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>碰见</rb><rt>pʽong-kyin</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>邪法</rb><rt>zia-fah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴耶稣</rb><rt>Pô-yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>总督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>士求</rb><rt>Z-gyiu</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>（<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>）<ruby><rb>凑队</rb><rt>tsʽeo-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>总督</rb><rt>Tsong-toh</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>扫罗</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>邪法</rb><rt>zia-fah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以吕马</rb><rt>Yi-li-mô</rt></ruby></u>（<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>翻出来</rb><rt>fæn-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>邪法</rb><rt>zia-fah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>」）<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>作对</rb><rt>tsôh-te</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>诱惑</rb><rt>yiu-ʽôh</rt><rb>总督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做过</rb><rt>tso-ko</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>扫罗</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u>（<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>*kyiao-leh*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>）<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>感动</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>*ngæn-tsing*</rt><rb>清盯</rb><rt>tsʽing-ting</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>10</sup> 「<ruby><rb>哎</rb><rt>ʽE</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>充满</rb><rt>cʽong-mun</rt><rb>百样</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>奸诈</rb><rt>kæn-tsô</rt><rb>恶毒</rb><rt>ôh-doh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>魔鬼个</rb><rt>mo-kwe-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>公义个</rb><rt>kong-yi-go</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>只管</rb><rt>coh-kwun</rt><rb>弄掉</rb><rt>long-diao</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>直</rb><rt>dzih</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>日头</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>*ngæn-tsing*</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>*ziang*</rt><rb>雾露</rb><rt>vu-lu</rt><rb>罩上</rb><rt>tsao-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>黑暗了</rb><rt>heh-en-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>摸来摸去</rb><rt>moh-læ-moh-kyʽi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>挡</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>总督</rb><rt>Tsong-toh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇起</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>相信了</rb><rt>siang-sing-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>PAO-LO</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>帕弗</rb><rt>Pʽô-feh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驶</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>旁非利亚</u>个</rb><rt>Bông-fi-li-üô-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>别加</rb><rt>Bih-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>别加</rb><rt>Bih-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走过去</rb><rt>tseo-ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>彼西底</u>个</rb><rt>Pe-si-ti-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>聚会堂里</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>律法</rb><rt>Lih-fah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>先知个</rb><rt>sin-cü-go</rt><rb>书</rb><rt>*shü*</rt><rb>读好仔</rb><rt>doh-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>聚会堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>劝化</rb><rt>kyʽün-hwô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立起身</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>抈抈</rb><rt>yüih-yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>*væn-pah*</rt><rb>怕惧</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>拣选</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>*ʽeo-deo*</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>出门</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>兴旺</rb><rt>hying-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>能干个</rb><rt>neng-ken-go</rt><rb>手梗</rb><rt>siu-kwang</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>毛</rb><rt>mao</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>大人</rb><rt>do-nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>抱</rb><rt>bao</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>七族个</rb><rt>tsʽih-dzoh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>设立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>判断官</rb><rt>pʽun-tön-kwun</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>毛</rb><rt>mao</rt><rb>四百</rb><rt>s-pah</rt><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒母耳</rb><rt>Sah-meo-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>设立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>便雅悯</rb><rt>Bin-yüô-ming</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇派个</rb><rt>keh-pʽa-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>基士</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-z-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>扫罗</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>*wông-ti*</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>*ʽEo-deo*</rt><rb>废掉</rb><rt>fi-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>举上</rb><rt>kyü-zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大辟</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶西</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-si-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大辟</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>合着</rb><rt>ʽeh-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>\[1\] *Peng-veng* *z-ngæn*, *yiu-teh* *doh*, kwʽun-shü. 本文字眼，有得读，宽恕。（原注）</sup>

<sup>23</sup> 「<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>应许个</rb><rt>ing-hyü-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>举上</rb><rt>kyü-zông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>救主</rb><rt>Kyiu-cü</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>出道</rb><rt>cʽih-dao</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>预先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>传开</rb><rt>djün-kʽæ</rt><rb>回心转意个</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i-go</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>职份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>猜</rb><rt>tsʽæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>*Kyi-toh*</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>后如</rb><rt>ʽeo-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>脚里个</rb><rt>kyiah-li-go</rt><rb>鞋带</rb><rt>ʽa-ta</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>欠</rb><rt>kyʽin</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>26</sup> 「<ruby><rb>众位</rb><rt>Cong-we</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>亚伯拉罕</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>*væn-pah*</rt><rb>怕惧</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>救个</rb><rt>kyiu-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>罪名</rb><rt>ze-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>应着</rb><rt>ing-djôh</rt><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>读个</rb><rt>doh-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>虽即</rb><rt>Se-tsih</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗出</rb><rt>feh-cʽih</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>*wa*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>树上</rb><rt>jü-zông</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>坟墓里</rb><rt>veng-mo-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>*ʽEo-deo*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>*yin-dzæ*</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> 「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>信息</rb><rt>sing-sih</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>预先</rb><rt>*yü-sin*</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>举上</rb><rt>kyü-zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>*ing-hyü*</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>第二首</rb><rt>di-nyi-siu</rt><rb>圣诗</rb><rt>Sing-s</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>写的个</rb><rt>sia-tih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>Kông-tao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>*si*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>*feh*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>*we*</rt><rb>烂糊</rb><rt>læn-wu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>*ing-hyü*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大辟</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>实在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一首</rb><rt>ih-siu</rt><rb>诗</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>烂糊</rb><rt>læn-wu</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大辟</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>*ʽeo-deo*</rt><rb>困熟</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安葬</rb><rt>en-tsông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>烂糊了</rb><rt>læn-wu-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>活转来个</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>烂糊</rb><rt>læn-wu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Peng-veng* *z-ngæn*, tseng-kyʽi z-ken, *z* *tsôh* eng-we *ka-shih*. 本文字眼，尊起事干，是作「恩惠」解说。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, gyi tsiao Jing-ming-go ts-i ka voh-z zi-go shü-dæ: *kʽen* S. 78. 72. 或话，其照神明个旨意介服侍自个世代，看 S. 78. 72.（原注）</sup>

<sup>38</sup> 「<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>看起来</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>众位</rb><rt>cong-we</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>赦罪个</rb><rt>sô-ze-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>*dao-li*</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>Kʽao-djôh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>脱出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>*ze*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>*feh*</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>*neng-keo*</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>脱出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>罪愆</rb><rt>*ze-kyʽin*</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>临着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：<sup>41</sup> 『<ruby><rb>轻慢个</rb><rt>Kyʽing-mæn-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>tsʽia-kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>惊骇</rb><rt>kying-hyiæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>灭亡</rb><rt>mih-vông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>打账</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人话</rb><rt>nying-wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt></ruby>。』 」

<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>聚会堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>下伐</rb><rt>ʽô-vah</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>*tsæ*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>聚会堂里个</rb><rt>Jü-we-dông-li-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>散开</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>虔心</rb><rt>gyin-sing</rt><rb>入</rb><rt>jih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>劝</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>守住</rb><rt>siu-djü</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Yiu-teh* *fæn*, keh loh nyih li-hyiang. 有得翻，箇六日里向。（原注）</sup>

<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>第二伐</rb><rt>Di-nyi-vah</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>差弗多</rb><rt>tsʽô-feh-to</rt><rb>咸城里</rb><rt>ʽen-zing-li</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聚拢来</rb><rt>jü-long-læ</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>妒忌</rb><rt>tu-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扳驳</rb><rt>pæn-poh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>非但</rb><rt>*fi-dæn*</rt><rb>扳驳</rb><rt>pæn-poh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>糟蹋</rb><rt>tsao-tʽah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>放胆</rb><rt>fông-tæn</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>原</rb><rt>*nyün*</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>定规</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>扭转</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djôh</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>设立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>别国人个</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying-go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>救星</rb><rt>kyiu-sing</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>地个</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>尽头</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt></ruby>。』 」

<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>别国人</rb><rt>Bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>称赞</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>定规</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>相信了</rb><rt>siang-sing-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>传遍</rb><rt>djün-pin</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>撺掇</rb><rt>tsʽön-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>虔心</rb><rt>gyin-sing</rt><rb>贵重个</rb><rt>kwe-djong-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>城里个</rb><rt>zing-li-go</rt><rb>乡绅</rb><rt>hyiang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>逼难</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>51</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>掸掉</rb><rt>tæn-diao</rt><rb>脚里个</rb><rt>kyiah-li-go</rt><rb>灰尘</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以哥念</rb><rt>Yi-ko-nyiæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>感动</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt></ruby>。
