### Sd. XIV.

<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>LÆ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以哥念</rb><rt>Yi-ko-nyiæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聚会堂里</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>道理</rb><rt>*dao-li*</rt><rb>讲勒</rb><rt>kông-leh</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>相信了</rb><rt>siang-sing-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>激动</rb><rt>kyih-dong</rt><rb>别国人个</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撺掇</rb><rt>tsʽön-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>恨</rb><rt>ʽeng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>庉勒</rb><rt>deng-leh</rt><rb>长久</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>放胆</rb><rt>fông-tæn</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>恩惠个</rb><rt>eng-we-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>城里个</rb><rt>zing-li-go</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>分开了</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ-de</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 46. 耶（稣）后（头）46（年）。（原注）</sup>

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>Teng-tao</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt><rb>拥上来</rb><rt>üong-zông-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>糟蹋</rb><rt>tsao-tʽah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞煞</rb><rt>kʽang-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>得知了</rb><rt>teh-cü-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>逃</rb><rt>dao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>吕高尼</u>个</rb><rt>Li-kao-nyi-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>路士得</rb><rt>Lu-z-teh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>特庇</rb><rt>Deh-pi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>*liang-go*</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>团圈个</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>路士得</rb><rt>LU-Z-TEH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>软</rb><rt>nyün</rt><rb>脚骨</rb><rt>kyiah-kweh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>坐间</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>娘胎</rb><rt>nyiang-tʽæ</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拐脚个</rb><rt>kwa-kyiah-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>清盯</rb><rt>tsʽing-ting</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>医好</rb><rt>i-hao</rt></ruby>，<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>响响</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>笔直</rb><rt>pih-dzih</rt><rb>立东</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跳起来</rb><rt>tʽiao-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走走</rb><rt>tseo-tseo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>吕高尼</rb><rt>Li-kao-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>土音</rb><rt>tʽu-ing</rt><rb>响响</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>现化</rb><rt>yin-hwô</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-lôh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>丢士</rb><rt>Tiu-z</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>称呼</rb><rt>*tsʽing-hwu*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希耳米</rb><rt>Hyi-r-mi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>领头</rb><rt>ling-deo</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>城外</rb><rt>Dzing-nga</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>丢士</rb><rt>Tiu-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>庙里个</rb><rt>*miao-li-go*</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>牵勒</rb><rt>kyʽin-leh</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>编出个</rb><rt>pin-cʽih-go</rt><rb>花</rb><rt>hwô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>祭祭</rb><rt>tsi-tsi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>使徒</rb><rt>S-du</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>听闻仔</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>扯开</rb><rt>cʽô-kʽæ</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>突然</rb><rt>deh-jün</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴起来</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>15</sup> 「<ruby><rb>朋友</rb><rt>Beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>性情</rb><rt>sing-dzing</rt><rb>相同个</rb><rt>siang-dong-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>虚花</rb><rt>hyü-hwô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>归服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>活个</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>万物</rb><rt>væn-veh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>以前个</rb><rt>Yi-zin-go</rt><rb>时世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>万国</rb><rt>væn-koh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>然而</rb><rt>jün-r</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>时常</rb><rt>*z-djông*</rt><rb>好好</rb><rt>hao-hao</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天里</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>赐落</rb><rt>s-lôh</rt><rb>雨</rb><rt>yü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>大熟个</rb><rt>da-joh-go</rt><rb>年成</rb><rt>nyin-dzing</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>口粮</rb><rt>kʽeo-liang</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>满心</rb><rt>mun-sing</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>Kông</rt><rb>箇番</rb><rt>keh-fæn</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽô</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>阻</rb><rt>tsu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗落</rb><rt>feh-lôh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>*kyi-go*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以哥念</rb><rt>Yi-ko-nyiæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撺掇</rb><rt>tsʽön-teh</rt><rb>众人个</rb><rt>cong-nying-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拖</rb><rt>tʽo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>城外</rb><rt>zing-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>道是</rb><rt>dao-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>团圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>围</rb><rt>yü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>东</rb><rt>tong</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>特庇</rb><rt>Deh-pi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>入教</rb><rt>jih-kyiao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>倒走转</rb><rt>tao-tseo-cün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>路士得</rb><rt>Lu-z-teh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>以哥念</rb><rt>Yi-ko-nyiæn</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>坚固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt><rb>门徒个</rb><rt>meng-du-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>劝</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>守住</rb><rt>siu-djü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>神国里</rb><rt>Jing-koh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djôh</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>难头</rb><rt>næn-deo</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>设立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>托付</rb><rt>tʽôh-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>穿过</rb><rt>cʽün-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼西底</rb><rt>Pe-si-ti</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>旁非利亚</rb><rt>Bông-fi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>别加</rb><rt>Bih-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚大利</rb><rt>Üô-da-li</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>驶</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>*hyüong-di*</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>*yin-dzæ*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做成个</rb><rt>tso-dzing-go</rt><rb>门份</rb><rt>meng-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>Kyi-jün</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>告诉</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>于是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉勒</rb><rt>deng-leh</rt><rb>长久</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu</rt></ruby>。

---

### Sd. XV.

<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>YIU</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落来</rb><rt>lôh-læ</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>规矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>。」<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>既经</rb><rt>kyi-kying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>争论</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt><rb>辩驳</rb><rt>bin-poh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>*cong-nying*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>定规</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>议论</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>教会</rb><rt>Kyiao-we</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓尼基</rb><rt>Fi-nyi-kyi</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>撒玛利亚</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>依路</rb><rt>*i-lu*</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>归服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>告诉</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>*cong-nying*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 51. 耶（稣）后（头）51（年）。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] Y. ʽE. 52. 耶（稣）后（头）52（年）。（原注）</sup>

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>（<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt></ruby>），<ruby><rb>立起身</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>*pih*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>*iao*</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] Yiu-teh tsʽeng keh-tsih shü z Pao-lo Pô-nô-pô djün En-di-ah z-ken. 有得忖箇节书是<u>保罗</u>、<u>巴拿巴</u>传<u>安提阿</u>事干。（原注）</sup>

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>使徒</rb><rt>S-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聚拢来</rb><rt>jü-long-læ</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt><rb>箇起</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>Dô-kô</rt><rb>辩论</rb><rt>bin-leng</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立起身</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>众位</rb><rt>Cong-we</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>拣选</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*ngô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>口里</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>众人个</rb><rt>*cong-nying-go*</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>*s-peh*</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>一样个</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>分别</rb><rt>feng-pih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>试试</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>揹弗动个</rb><rt>pe-feh-dong-go</rt><rb>轭</rb><rt>ah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>门徒个</rb><rt>meng-du-go</rt><rb>头颈</rb><rt>deo-kying</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>*tsih-yiu*</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>*ah-lah*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介个</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>响</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>详细</rb><rt>dziang-si</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>歇口</rb><rt>hyih-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>众位</rb><rt>Cong-we</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>才方</rb><rt>dzæ-fông</rt><rb>讲过</rb><rt>kông-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>咋样</rb><rt>dza-nyiang</rt><rb>看顾</rb><rt>kʽen-kwu</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>挑出</rb><rt>tʽiao-cʽih</rt><rb>一般</rb><rt>ih-pæn</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>奉</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>相对个</rb><rt>siang-te-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>16</sup> 『<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>转来</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>起好</rb><rt>kyʽi-hao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大辟</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>跌倒个</rb><rt>tih-tao-go</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>拆掉间个</rb><rt>tsʽah-diao-kæn-go</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>树起来</rb><rt>jü-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>直</rb><rt>dzih</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>剩落东个</rb><rt>dzing-lôh-tong-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>奉</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*Keh*</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>开辟</rb><rt>kʽæ-bih</rt><rb>以来</rb><rt>yi-læ</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>*sin*</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>吵扰</rb><rt>tsʽao-ziao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>归服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djôh</rt><rb>泥塑木雕个</rb><rt>nyi-su-moh-tiao-go</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>*tsi-veh*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>撮<sup> </sup>煞个</rb><rt>tsʽeh-sah-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>*sang-kʽeo*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>从古以来</rb><rt>dzong-kwu-yi-læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>书</rb><rt>*shü*</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>传开</rb><rt>djün-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聚会堂里</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>读个</rb><rt>doh-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] 勒；绳子等套住后用力拉紧</sup>

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>统</rb><rt>tʽong</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>决意</rb><rt>kyüih-i</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>拣选</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>*kyi-go*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聚队</rb><rt>jü-de</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴撒巴</rb><rt>Pô-sah-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>推尊个</rb><rt>tʽe-tseng-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>一封</rb><rt>ih-fong</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>带去</rb><rt>ta-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>使徒</rb><rt>S-du</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>写</rb><rt>*sia*</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>*sing*</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>叙利亚</rb><rt>Jü-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>基利家</rb><rt>Kyi-li-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>别国个</rb><rt>bih-koh-go</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>请请</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽing</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>吵扰</rb><rt>tsʽao-ziao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>摇惑</rb><rt>yiao-ʽôh</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。（<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>。）<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>同心合意</rb><rt>dong-sing-ʽeh-i</rt><rb>聚拢东</rb><rt>jü-long-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>决意</rb><rt>kyüih-i</rt><rb>拣选</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>*kyi-go*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>亲爱个</rb><rt>tsʽing-æ-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<sup>26</sup> （<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>舍出</rb><rt>sô-cʽih</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>口传</rb><rt>kʽeo-djün</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>决意</rb><rt>kyüih-i</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>别样</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt><rb>担头</rb><rt>tæn-deo</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>几样</rb><rt>kyi-yiang</rt><rb>要紧个</rb><rt>iao-kying-go</rt></ruby>，<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djôh</rt><rb>泥塑木雕个</rb><rt>nyi-su-moh-tiao-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>撮煞个</rb><rt>tsʽeh-sah-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>*sang-kʽeo*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好了</rb><rt>hao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>康健</rb><rt>kʽông-gyin</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇封</rb><rt>keh-fong</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>读好仔</rb><rt>doh-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>安慰个</rb><rt>en-w̆e-go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>劝劝</rb><rt>kyʽün-kyʽün</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坚固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>庉上</rb><rt>Deng-zông</rt><rb>几时</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>安安耽耽</rb><rt>en-en-tæn-tæn</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>定见</rb><rt>ding-kyin</rt><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>耽搁</rb><rt>tæn-kôh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>*hyüong-di*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>传开</rb><rt>djün-kʽæ</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, kyʽün-hwô-go. 或话，劝化个。（原注）</sup>

<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>KO-LEH</rt><rb>几</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>转去</rb><rt>cün-kyʽi</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>间了</rb><rt>kæn-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>（<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马可</rb><rt>Mô-kʽo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>）<ruby><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>旁非利亚</rb><rt>Bông-fi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>*dô-kô*</rt><rb>怏起来</rb><rt>ông-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>甚至于</rb><rt>zing-ts-ü</rt><rb>两下</rb><rt>liang-ʽô</rt><rb>分开</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马可</rb><rt>Mô-kʽo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>居比路</rb><rt>Kyü-pi-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>拣着</rb><rt>kæn-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>经过</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>叙利亚</rb><rt>Jü-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基利家</rb><rt>Kyi-li-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>坚固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 53. 耶（稣）后（头）53（年）。（原注）</sup>

### Sd. XVI.

<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>特庇</rb><rt>Deh-pi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>路士得</rb><rt>Lu-z-teh</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>提摩太</rb><rt>Di-mo-tʽa</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>路士得</rb><rt>Lu-z-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以哥念</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-ko-nyiæn-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>称赞</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶路撒冷</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng-go</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>定好个</rb><rt>ding-hao-go</rt><rb>规矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>守住</rb><rt>siu-djü</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>教会个</rb><rt>kyiao-we-go</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>*yü-kô*</rt><rb>坚固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>人数</rb><rt>nying-su</rt><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>加凑</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>YI-KYING</rt><rb>经过</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>弗吕家</rb><rt>Feh-li-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加拉太</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>禁住</rb><rt>kying-djü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>许</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚西亚</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>每西亚</rb><rt>Me-si-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>打账</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>庇推尼</rb><rt>Pi-tʽe-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>许</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>每西亚</rb><rt>Me-si-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>特罗亚</rb><rt>Deh-lo-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>夜头</rb><rt>Yia-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>显灵</rb><rt>hyin-ling</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马其顿</rb><rt>Mô-gyi-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>立东</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马其顿</rb><rt>Mô-gyi-teng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>帮助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>*Pao-lo*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>显灵</rb><rt>hyin-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马其顿</rb><rt>Mô-gyi-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>派来</rb><rt>pʽa-læ</rt><rb>实在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>特罗亚</rb><rt>Deh-lo-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>笔直</rb><rt>pih-dzih</rt><rb>驶</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒马特喇</rb><rt>Sah-mô-deh-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>尼亚波利</rb><rt>Nyi-üô-po-li</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓立比</rb><rt>Fi-lih-pi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马其顿</rb><rt>Mô-gyi-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一边</rb><rt>ih-pin</rt><rb>头一个</rb><rt>deo-ih-go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>*Lo-mô*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>新建个</rb><rt>sing-kyin-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Sia* ‘*S-du* *ying* *djün*’ *go* *Lu-kyia* *keh* *z-ʽeo* *teng* *Pao-lo* *dong-de-go*; *sô-yi* *wô*, *ah-lah*. 写〈使徒行传〉个<u>路加</u>箇时候等<u>保罗</u>同队个，所以话「阿拉」。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, di-ih pʽong-djôh-go. 或话，第一碰着个。（原注）</sup>

<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>庉上</rb><rt>deng-zông</rt><rb>几</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>城外</rb><rt>zing-nga</rt><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>聚拢间个</rb><rt>jü-long-kæn-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>吕底亚</rb><rt>Li-ti-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>推雅推喇</rb><rt>Tʽe-yüô-tʽe-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>买</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>紫花</rb><rt>ts-hwô</rt><rb>布</rb><rt>pu</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>*su-djông*</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>开导</rb><rt>kʽæ-dao</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>liu-sing</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>保罗</u>个</rb><rt>Pao-lo-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>和家</rb><rt>ʽo-kô</rt><rb>受了</rb><rt>ziu-leh</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>求恳</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>既经</rb><rt>kyi-kying</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>留</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>进去</rb><rt>tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>*ʽEo-deo*</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>丫头</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>邪鬼</rb><rt>zia-kyü</rt><rb>附的个</rb><rt>vu-tih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>起课</rb><rt>kyʽi-kʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>铜钿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>响响</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>得救个</rb><rt>teh-kyiu-go</rt><rb>路头</rb><rt>lu-deo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>特买</rb><rt>deh-ma</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>难熬</rb><rt>næn-ngao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扭转</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>鬼</rb><rt>*Kyü*</rt><rb>当时</rb><rt>tông-z</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出了</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>赚头个</rb><rt>dzæn-deo-go</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>失掉了</rb><rt>shih-diao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>拖</rb><rt>tʽo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>市面上</rb><rt>z-min-zông</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>解</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>吵乱</rb><rt>tsʽao-lön</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再话</rb><rt>*tsæ-wô*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>规矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>拥上来</rb><rt>üong-zông-læ</rt><rb>抵敌</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt><rb>脱掉</rb><rt>tʽeh-diao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>使</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>板子</rb><rt>pæn-ts</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>打了</rb><rt>Tang-leh</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>记数</rb><rt>kyi-su</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>挈落</rb><rt>kyʽih-lôh</rt><rb>牢监</rb><rt>lao-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>司狱司</rb><rt>s-nyüoh-s</rt><rb>牢靠</rb><rt>lao-kʽao</rt><rb>管守</rb><rt>kwun-siu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>号令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>挈</rb><rt>kyʽih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>牢底</rb><rt>lao-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>牢牢靠靠</rb><rt>lao-lao-kʽao-kʽao</rt><rb>上勒</rb><rt>zông-leh</rt><rb>大笼</rb><rt>do-long</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。
