<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>半夜过</rb><rt>Pun-yia-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>唱</rb><rt>tsʽông</rt><rb>诗</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>赞美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>听东</rb><rt>tʽing-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>忽然</rb><rt>Hweh-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>地震</rb><rt>di-cing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>牢监里个</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li-go</rt><rb>地基</rb><rt>di-kyi</rt><rb>摇动</rb><rt>yiao-dong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>登时</rb><rt>teng-z</rt><rb>开了</rb><rt>kʽæ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人个</rb><rt>kôh-nying-go</rt><rb>镣铐</rb><rt>liao-kʽao</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>褪落了</rb><rt>tʽeng-lôh-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>司狱司</rb><rt>S-nyüoh-s</rt><rb>调觉转来</rb><rt>diao-kao-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>牢监个</rb><rt>lao-kæn-go</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>开了</rb><rt>kʽæ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>道是</rb><rt>dao-z</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拔出</rb><rt>bah-cʽih</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>响响</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>司狱司</rb><rt>*S-nyüoh-s*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>亮</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>突然</rb><rt>deh-jün</rt><rb>走进去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>刮刮抖</rb><rt>gwah-gwah-teo</rt><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb><u>西拉</u>个</rb><rt>Si-lah-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>？」<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>和家</rb><rt>ʽo-kô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>。」<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屋里个</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>当时</rb><rt>Tông-z</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>司狱司</rb><rt>*s-nyüoh-s*</rt><rb>滰滰</rb><rt>gyiang-gyiang</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>伤痕</rb><rt>sông-ʽeng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>和家</rb><rt>ʽo-kô</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>陪</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>摆出</rb><rt>pa-cʽih</rt><rb>下饭</rb><rt>ʽô-væn</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>和家</rb><rt>ʽo-kô</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>欢欢喜喜</rb><rt>hwun-hwun-hyi-hyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>衙役</rb><rt>ngô-yüoh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>*tao*</rt><rb>司狱司</rb><rt>*s-nyüoh-s*</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>*u-sen*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>放出</rb><rt>fông-cʽih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>司狱司</rb><rt>S-nyüoh-s</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>：「<ruby><rb>官府</rb><rt>Kwun-fu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安安心心</rb><rt>en-en-sing-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>定罪过</rb><rt>ding-ze-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>挈落</rb><rt>kyʽih-lôh</rt><rb>牢监里</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*Keh*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt><rb>断断</rb><rt>tön-tön</rt><rb>使弗得个</rb><rt>s-feh-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>陪</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。」<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>衙役</rb><rt>Ngô-yüoh</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>回报</rb><rt>we-pao</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>官府</rb><rt>*Kwun-fu*</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吃𢞕了</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>劝</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>陪</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*Keh*</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>*liang-go*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>牢监</rb><rt>lao-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>吕底亚</u>个</rb><rt>Li-ti-üô-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>*oh-li*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>见了</rb><rt>kyin-leh</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>劝劝</rb><rt>kyʽün-kyʽün</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

---

### Sd. XVII.

<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>GYI-LAH</rt><rb>经过</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>暗妃波里</rb><rt>En-fi-po-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚波罗尼亚</rb><rt>Üô-po-lo-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>帖撒罗尼迦</rb><rt>Tʽiah-sah-lo-nyi-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>聚会堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>老例</rb><rt>lao-li</rt><rb>走进去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一连</rb><rt>ih-lin</rt><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-leh</rt><rb>三个</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>圣书</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>解说</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>明明</rb><rt>ming-ming</rt><rb>靠实</rb><rt>kʽao-jih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>难</rb><rt>næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>内中</rb><rt>Nen-cong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>劝醒了</rb><rt>kyʽün-sing-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>亲近</rb><rt>tsʽing-gying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>虔心个</rb><rt>gyin-sing-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>贵重个</rb><rt>kwe-djong-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>少</rb><rt>siao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>妒忌心</rb><rt>tu-gyi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>市面上</rb><rt>z-min-zông</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>无赖</rb><rt>vu-la</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>摇拢</rb><rt>yiao-long</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吵乱</rb><rt>tsʽao-lön</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>突然</rb><rt>deh-jün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶孙</rb><rt>Yiæ-seng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>碰弗着</rb><rt>Pʽong-feh-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拖勒</rb><rt>tʽo-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶孙</rb><rt>Yiæ-seng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>怀怀响</rb><rt>wæ-wæ-hyiang</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>反乱</rb><rt>fæn-lön</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>来了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶孙</rb><rt>Yiæ-seng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>*wa*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>收留</rb><rt>siu-liu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>违背</rb><rt>we-be</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>该撒</u>个</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-go</rt><rb>号令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>吃𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>官府</rb><rt>*Kwun-fu*</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶孙</rb><rt>Yiæ-seng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>余多个</rb><rt>yü-to-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>取保</rb><rt>cʽü-pao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>当夜</rb><rt>tông-yia</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>庇哩亚</rb><rt>Pi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>聚会堂里</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>*Yiu-tʽa*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好如</rb><rt>hao-jü</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>帖撒罗尼迦</rb><rt>Tʽiah-sah-lo-nyi-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一心</rb><rt>ih-sing</rt><rb>情愿</rb><rt>dzing-nyün</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>考究</rb><rt>kʽao-kyiu</rt><rb>圣书</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>内中</rb><rt>nen-cong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>相信了</rb><rt>siang-sing-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>贵重个</rb><rt>kwe-djong-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>少</rb><rt>siao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>帖撒罗尼迦</rb><rt>Tʽiah-sah-lo-nyi-kyüô</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>庇哩亚</rb><rt>Pi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>吵乱</rb><rt>tsʽao-lön</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>海口</rb><rt>hæ-kʽeo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>提摩太</rb><rt>Di-mo-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>陪</rb><rt>Be</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅典</rb><rt>Yüô-tin</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>提摩太</rb><rt>Di-mo-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>速速</rb><rt>soh-soh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>归了</rb><rt>kyü-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>PAO-LO</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅典</rb><rt>Yüô-tin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>间</rb><rt>kæn</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>满</rb><rt>mun</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>泥塑木雕</rb><rt>nyi-su-moh-tiao</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>着急了</rb><rt>dziah-kyih-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聚会堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>虔心</rb><rt>gyin-sing</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>辩论</rb><rt>bin-leng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>每日</rb><rt>me-nyih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>市面上</rb><rt>z-min-zông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>碰着个</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>*yia*</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 54. 耶（稣）后（头）54（年）。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, ʽeh-zing-li tu cün-meng kong-vong nyi-su-moh-tiao. 或话，合城里都专门供奉泥塑木雕。（原注）</sup>

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以彼古罗</rb><rt>Yi-pe-kwu-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>士多亚</u>个</rb><rt>Z-to-üô-go</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>扳驳</rb><rt>pæn-poh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有得个</rb><rt>Yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>多讲多话</rb><rt>to-kông-to-wô</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>有得个</rb><rt>Yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>恶像</rb><rt>ôh-ziang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>外国</rb><rt>nga-koh</rt><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>*Pao-lo*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>再活个</rb><rt>tsæ-weh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>*Cong-nying*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚略巴古</rb><rt>Üô-liah-pô-kwu</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>新鲜</rb><rt>sing-sin</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几样</rb><rt>kyi-yiang</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>。」<sup>21</sup> （<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅典</rb><rt>Yüô-tin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>出门人</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>别样</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>单</rb><rt>tæn</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>讲讲</rb><rt>kông-kông</rt><rb>听听</rb><rt>tʽing-tʽing</rt><rb>新鲜</rb><rt>sing-sin</rt><rb>行情</rb><rt>ʽông-dzing</rt></ruby>。）

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, ʽô-tsôh pʽe-ts. 或话，下作坯子。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *Ziu-z*, Üô-liah sæn. 就是，<u>亚略</u>山。（原注）</sup>

<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚略巴古</rb><rt>Üô-liah-pô-kwu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>众位</rb><rt>Cong-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅典</rb><rt>Yüô-tin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>才始</rb><rt>dzæ-s</rt><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>拜个</rb><rt>pa-go</rt><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>祭坛</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>字</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>未</rb><rt>VI</rt><rb>识之</rb><rt>SIH-TS</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>JING</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>*wa*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>供奉</rb><rt>kong-vong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>*hyiao-teh*</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>Keh-we</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>宇宙</rb><rt>yü-dziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>万物</rb><rt>væn-veh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>天地个</rb><rt>tʽin-di-go</rt><rb>主宰</rb><rt>Cü-tsæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>*nying-go*</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>起个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>庙宇里</rb><rt>miao-yü-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>供应</rb><rt>kong-ing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恶像</rb><rt>*ôh-ziang*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>缺少长短</rb><rt>kyʽüih-siao-dziang-tön</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生气</rb><rt>seng-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>根脉</rb><rt>keng-mah</rt><rb>造出</rb><rt>zao-cʽih</rt><rb>万国个</rb><rt>væn-koh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>各到处</rb><rt>kôh-tao-cʽü</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>面顶</rb><rt>min-teng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>预先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>限定个</rb><rt>ʽæn-ding-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉个</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>址界</rb><rt>ts-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>铸就好</rb><rt>cü-dziu-hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>寻寻</rb><rt>zing-zing</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>摸来摸去</rb><rt>moh-læ-moh-kyʽi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>寻着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>然而</rb><rt>jün-r</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>实在</rb><rt>*jih-dzæ*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>离</rb><rt>li</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>远</rb><rt>yün</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>行动</rb><rt>ʽang-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>诗</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>生个</rb><rt>sang-go</rt></ruby>。』<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>Kyi-jün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>生个</rb><rt>sang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>体质</rb><rt>tʽi-tsih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>手段</rb><rt>siu-dön</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>灵巧</rb><rt>ling-kyʽiao</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>雕刻个</rb><rt>tiao-kʽeh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>以前</rb><rt>*Yi-zin*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白个</rb><rt>ming-bah-go</rt><rb>时世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>眼开眼闭</rb><rt>ngæn-kʽæ-ngæn-pi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>各到各处</rb><rt>kôh-tao-kôh-cʽü</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>限</rb><rt>ʽæn</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>定规个</rb><rt>ding-kwe-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, pa bu-sah go z-ken. 或话，拜菩萨个事干。（原注）</sup>

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>*Keh-sing*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>活转来个</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>取笑</rb><rt>cʽü-siao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。」<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是介</rb><rt>ziu-z-ka</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>相信了</rb><rt>siang-sing-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>内中</rb><rt>nen-cong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚略巴古</rb><rt>Üô-liah-pô-kwu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>丢尼修</rb><rt>Tiu-nyi-siu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大马哩</rb><rt>Da-mô-li</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt></ruby>。

---

### Sd. XVIII.

<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>KEH-SING</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅典</rb><rt>Yüô-tin</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥林多</rb><rt>Ko-ling-to</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>Pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚居拉</rb><rt>Üô-kyü-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>本都</rb><rt>Peng-tu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>百基拉</rb><rt>Pah-kyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>刚刚</rb><rt>kông-kông</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革老丢</rb><rt>Keh-lao-tiu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>号令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>京城</rb><rt>*kying-dzing*</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>*Pao-lo*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做样</rb><rt>tso-yiang</rt><rb>手艺</rb><rt>siu-nyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>业</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>*Pao-lo*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>每逢</rb><rt>me-vong</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聚会堂里</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>劝劝</rb><rt>kyʽün-kyʽün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>Teng-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>提摩太</rb><rt>Di-mo-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马其顿</rb><rt>Mô-gyi-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>着急</rb><rt>dziah-kyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>扳驳</rb><rt>pæn-poh</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>亵渎个</rb><rt>sih-doh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>*Pao-lo*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>㪗㪗</rb><rt>tʽeo-tʽeo</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔自个</rb><rt>ng-zi-go</rt><rb>头上</rb><rt>deo-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>干净个</rb><rt>ken-zing-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>从今以后</rb><rt>dzong-kying-yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>一份</rb><rt>ih-veng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹士都</rb><rt>Yiu-z-tu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>聚会堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>铁隔壁</rb><rt>tʽih-kah-pih</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>管</rb><rt>Kwun</rt><rb>聚会堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革哩士布</rb><rt>Keh-li-z-pu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>*zi*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>和家</rb><rt>ʽo-kô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>哥林多</rb><rt>Ko-ling-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>*dao-li*</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>夜到</rb><rt>Yia-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>显灵</rb><rt>hyin-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>Hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>歇口</rb><rt>hyih-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>下手</rb><rt>ʽô-siu</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-lôh</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>*keh-deo*</rt><rb>一年</rb><rt>ih-nyin</rt><rb>零</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>六个</rb><rt>loh-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打账</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>迦流</rb><rt>Kyüô-liu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>公道</rb><rt>kong-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奸刁个</rb><rt>kæn-tiao-go</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>门份</rb><rt>meng-veng</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>耐心</rb><rt>næ-sing</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>*tʽing*</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>*Pao-lo*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>聚会堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>别开</rb><rt>bih-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>快到个</rb><rt>kwʽa-tao-go</rt><rb>节肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>肯个</rb><rt>kʽeng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>转来</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒利亚</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>*Yiæ-lu-sah-leng*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>*ʽeo-deo*</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>*Læ*</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>*keh-deo*</rt><rb>庉上</rb><rt>deng-zông</rt><rb>几时</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>挨次序</rb><rt>a-tsʽ-jü</rt><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加拉太</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>弗吕家</rb><rt>Feh-li-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坚固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>门徒个</rb><rt>meng-du-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚波罗</rb><rt>Üô-po-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚力山太</rb><rt>Üô-lih-sæn-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>口才</rb><rt>kʽeo-dzæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>圣书</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>通透</rb><rt>tʽong-tʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>学习</rb><rt>ʽôh-jih</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>热心</rb><rt>nyih-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>仔细</rb><rt>ts-si</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约翰</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聚会堂里</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>放胆</rb><rt>fông-tæn</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>亚居拉</rb><rt>Üô-kyü-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>百基拉</rb><rt>Pah-kyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>听闻仔</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>留</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>归去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>解说</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>仔细</rb><rt>ts-si</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亚波罗</rb><rt>Üô-po-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚该亚</rb><rt>Üô-kæ-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>劝</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>*keh-deo*</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>Kyi-jün</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>帮助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>众人个</rb><rt>cong-nying-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>血心</rb><rt>hyüih-sing</rt><rb>驳倒</rb><rt>poh-tao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>圣书</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>表明</rb><rt>piao-ming</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。

### Sd. XIX.

<u><ruby><rb>亚波罗</rb><rt>ÜÔ-PO-LO</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥林多</rb><rt>Ko-ling-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>上路</rb><rt>zông-lu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>Pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>受了</rb><rt>ziu-leh</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>赐落来</rb><rt>*s-lôh-læ*</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>听闻过</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ko</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>*Pao-lo*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>约翰</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>回心转意个</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i-go</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>后如</rb><rt>ʽeo-jü</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>听闻仔</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>奉</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>临着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>当时</rb><rt>*tông-z*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>各处</rb><rt>kôh-cʽü</rt><rb>土话</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>未来</rb><rt>mi-læ</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>*Pao-lo*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>聚会堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>放胆</rb><rt>fông-tæn</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>三个</rb><rt>sæn-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>议论</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt><rb>神国个</rb><rt>Jing-koh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>劝劝</rb><rt>kyʽün-kyʽün</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>心肠</rb><rt>sing-dziang</rt><rb>石硬</rb><rt>zah-ngang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>谤毁</rb><rt>pông-hwe</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>*Pao-lo*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>讴出</rb><rt>eo-cʽih</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>*zi*</rt><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>推喇奴</u>个</rb><rt>Tʽe-lah-nu-go</rt><rb>书房里</rb><rt>shü-vông-li</rt><rb>辩论</rb><rt>bin-leng</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇种介</rb><rt>Keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚西亚</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>保罗</u>个</rb><rt>Pao-lo-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt><rb>格外个</rb><rt>kah-nga-go</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>甚至于</rb><rt>zing-ts-ü</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>手巾</rb><rt>siu-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>围裙</rb><rt>yü-gyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>保罗</u>个</rb><rt>Pao-lo-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>病人个</rb><rt>bing-nying-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>脱掉了</rb><rt>tʽeh-diao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恶鬼</rb><rt>ôh-kyü</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出了</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>走江湖</rb><rt>tseo-kông-wu</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>业</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>恶鬼</rb><rt>ôh-kyü</rt><rb>附的个</rb><rt>vu-tih-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>传个</rb><rt>djün-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>*tseo-cʽih-kyʽi*</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>士基华</rb><rt>Z-kyi-wô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>七个</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>恶鬼</rb><rt>Ôh-kyü</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>认得个</rb><rt>nying-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>？」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>恶鬼</rb><rt>Ôh-kyü</rt><rb>附的个</rb><rt>vu-tih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跳过去</rb><rt>tʽiao-ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打败</rb><rt>tang-ba</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>制服</rb><rt>cü-voh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出膊</rb><rt>cʽih-poh</rt><rb>受伤</rb><rt>ziu-sông</rt><rb>逃出</rb><rt>dao-cʽih</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>相信个</rb><rt>Siang-sing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>明当当</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt><rb>招认</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>详细</rb><rt>dziang-si</rt><rb>话出来</rb><rt>wô-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>邪法</rb><rt>zia-fah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>驮拢</rb><rt>do-long</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>书</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>𤈦掉</rb><rt>me-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>估估</rb><rt>kwu-kwu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>价钿</rb><rt>kô-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>足值</rb><rt>tsoh-dzih</rt><rb>五千两</rb><rt>ng-tsʽin-*liang*</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>行开</rb><rt>ʽang-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>得胜</rb><rt>teh-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>KEH-SING</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>做好了</rb><rt>tso-hao-de</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>定见</rb><rt>ding-kyin</rt><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马其顿</rb><rt>Mô-gyi-teng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亚该亚</rb><rt>Üô-kæ-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>帮手</rb><rt>pông-siu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>提摩太</rb><rt>Di-mo-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以拉都</rb><rt>Yi-lah-tu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马其顿</rb><rt>Mô-gyi-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>*wa*</rt><rb>暂庉</rb><rt>dzæn-deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚西亚</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 59. 耶（稣）后（头）59（年）。（原注）</sup>

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>闹热</rb><rt>nao-nyih</rt><rb>非凡</rb><rt>fi-væn</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>银师父</rb><rt>nying-s-vu</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>底米丢</rb><rt>Ti-mi-tiu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚底米</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-ti-mi-go</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>神堂</rb><rt>jing-dông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>伙计</rb><rt>ho-kyi</rt><rb>铜钿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt><rb>赚了</rb><rt>dzæn-leh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>少</rb><rt>siao</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>讴拢</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星介</rb><rt>keh-sing-ka</rt><rb>行当</rb><rt>ʽông-tông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>师父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>朋友</rb><rt>Beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠</rb><rt>kʽao</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>行当</rb><rt>ʽông-tông</rt><rb>得利</rb><rt>teh-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>非但</rb><rt>fi-dæn</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>差弗多</rb><rt>tsʽô-feh-to</rt><rb>统</rb><rt>tʽong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚西亚</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>劝醒</rb><rt>kyʽün-sing</rt><rb>带掉</rb><rt>ta-diao</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>*nying-go*</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>算弗得</rb><rt>sön-feh-teh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事业</rb><rt>z-nyih</rt><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>看轻</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚底米</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-ti-mi-go</rt><rb>庙宇</rb><rt>miao-yü</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>*kʽong-pʽô*</rt><rb>化为乌有</rb><rt>hwô-we-u-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>统</rb><rt>tʽong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚西亚</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>遍天下</rb><rt>pin-tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>供奉</rb><rt>kong-vong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚底米</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-ti-mi-go</rt><rb>威严</rb><rt>w̆e-nyin</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>听闻仔</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ts</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>怀怀响</rb><rt>wæ-wæ-hyiang</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚底米</rb><rt>Üô-ti-mi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大勒紧</rb><rt>do-leh-kying</rt></ruby>！」<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>咸城里</rb><rt>ʽEn-zing-li</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>闹闹热热</rb><rt>nao-nao-nyih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抲勒</rb><rt>kʽô-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马其顿</rb><rt>Mô-gyi-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦犹</rb><rt>Kyüô-yiu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚里达古</rb><rt>Üô-li-dah-kwu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>出门个</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>斩齐</rb><rt>tsæn-zi</rt><rb>拥进</rb><rt>üong-tsing</rt><rb>戏文场里</rb><rt>hyi-veng-dziang-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚西亚</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>宗伯</rb><rt>tsong-pah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt></ruby>（<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>），<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>劝</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>断</rb><rt>tön</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>戏文场里</rb><rt>hyi-veng-dziang-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>胡咙响亮</rb><rt>wu-long-hyiang-liang</rt><rb>纷纷不一</rb><rt>feng-feng-peh-ih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>聚拢东个</rb><rt>jü-long-tong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>乱撺去</rb><rt>lön-tsʽön-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大半</rb><rt>ih-do-pun</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>走拢来个</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚力山大</rb><rt>Üô-lih-sæn-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>推出来</rb><rt>tʽe-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>*yiu-sing*</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>立带起</rb><rt>lih-tæn-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>亚力山大</rb><rt>Üô-lih-sæn-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>抈抈</rb><rt>yüih-yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>分剖</rb><rt>feng-pʽeo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>合口齐动</rb><rt>ʽeh-kʽeo-zi-dong</rt><rb>讴起来</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚底米</rb><rt>Üô-ti-mi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大勒紧</rb><rt>do-leh-kying</rt></ruby>！」<ruby><rb>箇种介</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>大约</rb><rt>da-iah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>时辰</rb><rt>z-zing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>县官</rb><rt>yün-kwun</rt><rb>劝息</rb><rt>kyʽün-sih</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚底米</rb><rt>Üô-ti-mi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>降落来个</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ-go</rt><rb>神像</rb><rt>*jing-ziang*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>庙头</rb><rt>miao-deo</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>？<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>扳驳</rb><rt>pæn-poh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>静带落</rb><rt>zing-tæn-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>乱七八糟</rb><rt>lön-tsʽih-pah-tsao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>犯着</rb><rt>væn-djôh</rt><rb>庙宇</rb><rt>miao-yü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>亵渎</rb><rt>sih-doh</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>竟是介</rb><rt>kying-z-ka</rt><rb>拖勒来</rb><rt>tʽo-leh-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>底米丢</rb><rt>Ti-mi-tiu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>师父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>告状个</rb><rt>kao-zông-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>*nyih-ts*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>两下</rb><rt>liang-ʽô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>官司</rb><rt>kwun-s</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>别起</rb><rt>bih-kyʽi</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>合例个</rb><rt>ʽeh-li-go</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>决断</rb><rt>kyüih-tön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>Kông-tao</rt><rb>今末个</rb><rt>kyih-mih-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>*z-ken*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>反乱</rb><rt>fæn-lön</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>分剖</rb><rt>feng-pʽeo</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>闹事</rb><rt>nao-z</rt></ruby>。」<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>讲好</rb><rt>Kông-hao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>发散</rb><rt>fah-sæn</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, z kong-vong gyi go. 或话，是供奉其个。（原注）</sup>

### Sd. XX.

<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>KEH-GO</rt><rb>吵乱</rb><rt>tsʽao-lön</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>安静了</rb><rt>en-zing-de</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讴拢</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分别</rb><rt>feng-bih</rt><rb>一番</rb><rt>ih-fæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马其顿</rb><rt>Mô-gyi-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>经过</rb><rt>Kying-ko</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>劝劝</rb><rt>kyʽün-kyʽün</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇头</rb><rt>*Keh-deo*</rt><rb>庉上</rb><rt>deng-zông</rt><rb>三个</rb><rt>sæn-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>叙利亚</rb><rt>Jü-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>设</rb><rt>shih</rt><rb>计谋</rb><rt>kyi-meo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>决意</rb><rt>kyüih-i</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马其顿</rb><rt>Mô-gyi-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走转来</rb><rt>tseo-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>Keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚西亚</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>庇哩亚</u>个</rb><rt>Pi-li-üô-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所巴德</rb><rt>Sô-pô-teh</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb><u>帖撒罗尼迦</u>个</rb><rt>Tʽiah-sah-lo-nyi-kyüô-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚里达古</rb><rt>Üô-li-dah-kwu</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西公都</rb><rt>Si-kong-tu</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb><u>特庇</u>个</rb><rt>Deh-pi-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦犹</rb><rt>Kyüô-yiu</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>提摩太</rb><rt>Di-mo-tʽa</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb><u>亚西亚</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>推基古</rb><rt>Tʽe-kyi-kwu</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>特罗非摩</rb><rt>Deh-lo-fi-mo</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>Keh-pæn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>特罗亚</rb><rt>Deh-lo-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>间</rb><rt>kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>无酵节</rb><rt>Vu-kao-tsih</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>日子个</rb><rt>nyih-ts-go</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓立比</rb><rt>Fi-lih-pi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>五</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>特罗亚</rb><rt>Deh-lo-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>会着</rb><rt>we-djôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>庉上</rb><rt>deng-zông</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。
