<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>七日个</rb><rt>Tsʽih-nyih-go</rt><rb>头日</rb><rt>deo-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>聚拢来</rb><rt>jü-long-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>𤖼</rb><rt>pʽah</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打账</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>讲勒</rb><rt>kông-leh</rt><rb>长篇</rb><rt>dziang-pʽin</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>半夜过</rb><rt>pun-yia-ko</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聚拢间个</rb><rt>jü-long-kæn-go</rt><rb>楼顶</rb><rt>leo-teng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>灯亮</rb><rt>teng-liang</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>后生</rb><rt>ʽeo-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹推古</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽe-kwu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>槛头</rb><rt>kʽæn-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>瞌𥅻</rb><rt>kʽeh-cʽong</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲勒</rb><rt>kông-leh</rt><rb>长久</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>煞觉</rb><rt>sah-kao</rt><rb>困熟</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>三层楼</rb><rt>sæn-zeng-leo</rt><rb>跌落来</rb><rt>tih-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>挡起</rb><rt>tông-kyʽi</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走落来</rb><rt>tseo-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扑倒</rb><rt>pʽoh-tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>捼勒</rb><rt>o-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>吃𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>走上去</rb><rt>tseo-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𤖼勒</rb><rt>pʽah-leh</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>一餐</rb><rt>*ih-tsʽæn*</rt><rb>饭</rb><rt>*væn*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>讲勒</rb><rt>kông-leh</rt><rb>长久</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>后生</rb><rt>ʽeo-sang</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>活东了</rb><rt>weh-tong-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心里</rb><rt>*sing-li*</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>安慰了</rb><rt>en-w̆e-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚朔</rb><rt>Üô-shoh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>接</rb><rt>tsih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>定见</rb><rt>ding-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>步行勒</rb><rt>bu-ʽang-leh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚朔</rb><rt>Üô-shoh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>会着</rb><rt>we-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>*Pao-lo*</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>接</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驶</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米推利尼</rb><rt>Mi-tʽe-li-nyi</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>驶</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基阿</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-ah-go</rt><rb>对面</rb><rt>te-min</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>驶进</rb><rt>s-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒摩</rb><rt>Sah-mo</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>*ʽeo-deo*</rt><rb>泊</rb><rt>boh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>多基脸</rb><rt>To-kyi-lin</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>第四</rb><rt>di-s</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米利都</rb><rt>Mi-li-tu</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>定见</rb><rt>ding-kyin</rt><rb>驶过</rb><rt>s-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚西亚</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>耽搁</rb><rt>tæn-kôh</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>好使得个</rb><rt>hao-s-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>巴弗能够</rb><rt>pô-feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>五旬节</rb><rt>wu-shing-tsih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>*PAO-LO*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米利都</rb><rt>Mi-li-tu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>想请</rb><rt>siang-tsʽing</rt><rb>教会里</rb><rt>kyiao-we-li</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>来了</rb><rt>Læ-de</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚西亚</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>头一日</rb><rt>deo-ih-nyih</rt><rb>以来</rb><rt>yi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>谦虚</rb><rt>kyʽin-hyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>长</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>眼泪</rb><rt>ngæn-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>谋害</rb><rt>meo-ʽæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>难头</rb><rt>næn-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>关着</rb><rt>kwæn-djôh</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>*ng-lah-go*</rt><rb>益处</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>隐瞒</rb><rt>ing-mun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>血心</rb><rt>hyüih-sing</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>劝</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt><rb>归服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>22</sup> 「<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>缚住了个</rb><rt>bo-djü-liao-go</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>刑具</rb><rt>ying-gyü</rt><rb>祸难</rb><rt>ʽo-næn</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>间</rb><rt>kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>*keh-sing*</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>*z-ken*</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>着</rb><rt>dziah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>意里</rb><rt>i-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>弗舍得</rb><rt>feh-sô-teh</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>高高兴兴</rb><rt>kao-kao-hying-hying</rt><rb>走完</rb><rt>tseo-wun</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>路程</rb><rt>lu-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>职份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>恩惠个</rb><rt>eng-we-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>*su-djông*</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>走来走去</rb><rt>tseo-læ-tseo-kyʽi</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>神国个</rb><rt>Jing-koh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>以后</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>面</rb><rt>min</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>众人个</rb><rt>cong-nying-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>隐瞒</rb><rt>ing-mun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>设立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>监督</rb><rt>kyin-toh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一队</rb><rt>ih-de</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>照管</rb><rt>tsiao-kwun</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>买来个</rb><rt>ma-læ-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>厉害个</rb><rt>li-ʽæ-go</rt><rb>豺狼</rb><rt>za-lông</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>弗舍得</rb><rt>feh-sô-teh</rt><rb>箇队</rb><rt>keh-de</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>歪斜个</rb><rt>hwa-zia-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>引诱</rb><rt>ying-yiu</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>警醒</rb><rt>kying-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>记得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>日夜</rb><rt>nyih-yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>歇</rb><rt>hyih</rt><rb>含勒</rb><rt>ʽen-leh</rt><rb>眼泪</rb><rt>ngæn-li</rt><rb>劝</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> 「<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>*tʽôh*</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>恩惠个</rb><rt>eng-we-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>成就</rb><rt>dzing-dziu</rt><rb>尔拉德气</rb><rt>ng-lah-teh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>尊起了</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi-liao</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看相</rb><rt>kʽen-siang</rt><rb>谁个</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>箇双</rb><rt>keh-shông</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>供应</rb><rt>kong-ing</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>用场</rb><rt>yüong-dziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>供应</rb><rt>*kong-ing*</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>摆出</rb><rt>pa-cʽih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>劳碌</rb><rt>lao-loh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>帮助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>懦弱个</rb><rt>no-ziah-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>记得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>施</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>恩</rb><rt>eng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>恩</rb><rt>eng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>讲好仔</rb><rt>Kông-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跪落</rb><rt>gyü-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>哭起来</rb><rt>kʽoh-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>捼勒</rb><rt>o-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>保罗</u>个</rb><rt>Pao-lo-go</rt><rb>头颈</rb><rt>deo-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嗅嗅</rb><rt>hyüong-hyüong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>嘴</rb><rt>cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>忧闷</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>以后</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>面</rb><rt>min</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>。

### Sd. XXI.

<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>AH-LAH</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>别开</rb><rt>bih-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>笔直</rb><rt>pih-dzih</rt><rb>驶</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥士</rb><rt>Ko-z</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗底</rb><rt>Lo-ti</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>帕大喇</rb><rt>Pʽô-da-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>Pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓尼基</rb><rt>Fi-nyi-kyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>开出去</rb><rt>kʽæ-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>Kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>居比路</rb><rt>Kyü-pi-lu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>顺手边</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt><rb>驶过</rb><rt>s-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>叙利亚</rb><rt>Jü-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>推罗</rb><rt>Tʽe-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>抛</rb><rt>pʽao</rt><rb>锚</rb><rt>mao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>卸载</rb><rt>sia-zæ</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>寻着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt><rb>箇头个</rb><rt>*keh-deo-go*</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>庉上</rb><rt>deng-zông</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>感动</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 卸船，《字语汇解》作「卸⿰舟载」</sup>

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>日子</rb><rt>Nyih-ts</rt><rb>满了</rb><rt>mun-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>老婆</rb><rt>lao-bo</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>城外</rb><rt>zing-nga</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海涂上</rb><rt>hæ-du-zông</rt><rb>跪落</rb><rt>gyü-lôh</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>别开</rb><rt>bih-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>推罗</rb><rt>Tʽe-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>驶</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>多利买</rb><rt>To-li-mæ</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>船路</rb><rt>jün-lu</rt><rb>完了</rb><rt>wun-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>*keh-deo*</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉上</rb><rt>deng-zông</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>同队个</rb><rt>dong-de-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒利亚</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>腓力</u>个</rb><rt>Fi-lih-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-lôh</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>七个</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>在内个</rb><rt>dzæ-nen-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四个</rb><rt>s-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>童女</rb><rt>dong-nyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>庉上</rb><rt>deng-zông</rt><rb>好几</rb><rt>hao-kyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚迦布</rb><rt>Üô-kyüô-pu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>Tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>保罗</u>个</rb><rt>Pao-lo-go</rt><rb>缴身带</rb><rt>kyiao-sing-ta</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>缚</rb><rt>bo</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>箇根</rb><rt>Keh-keng</rt><rb>带个</rb><rt>ta-go</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>缚</rb><rt>bo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>别国人个</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>Tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>劝</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>啼哭</rb><rt>di-kʽoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>伤</rb><rt>sông</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>缚</rb><rt>bo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>情愿个</rb><rt>dzing-nyün-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>劝</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歇作</rb><rt>hyih-tsôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>几</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>备好</rb><rt>be-hao</rt><rb>铺盖</rb><rt>pʽu-kæ</rt><rb>行李</rb><rt>ʽang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>*kyi-go*</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>该撒利亚</u>个</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-li-üô-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>老</rb><rt>lao</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>居比路</rb><rt>Kyü-pi-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>拿孙</rb><rt>Nô-seng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>庉处</rb><rt>deng-cʽü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>AH-LAH</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>高高兴兴</rb><rt>kao-kao-hying-hying</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>进去</rb><rt>tsing-kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>*Pao-lo*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>别国</rb><rt>bih-koh</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>一打一</rb><rt>ih-tang-ih</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>听闻仔</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>赞美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>万数</rb><rt>væn-su</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>热心个</rb><rt>nyih-sing-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>长通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>别国</rb><rt>bih-koh</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>老</rb><rt>*lao*</rt><rb>规矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>弄法</rb><rt>long-fah</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>聚拢来</rb><rt>jü-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到东了</rb><rt>tao-tong-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四个</rb><rt>s-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>愿心</rb><rt>nyün-sing</rt><rb>许间</rb><rt>hyü-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>领勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>驮出</rb><rt>do-cʽih</rt><rb>费用</rb><rt>fi-yüong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>剃</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>虚个</rb><rt>hyü-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>守住</rb><rt>siu-djü</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>Kông-tao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>定规</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>守住</rb><rt>siu-djü</rt><rb>箇星介</rb><rt>keh-sing-ka</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djôh</rt><rb>泥塑木雕个</rb><rt>nyi-su-moh-tiao-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>撮煞个</rb><rt>tsʽeh-sah-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>*sang-kʽeo*</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] 原书此处错印为 kong-tao，据文意及 1898 年版改正</sup>

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>领勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>洁净仔</rb><rt>kyih-zing-ts</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>洁净个</rb><rt>kyih-zing-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>满了</rb><rt>mun-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>单等</rb><rt>tæn-teng</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>完了</rb><rt>wun-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚西亚</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>*læ-go*</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弄勒</rb><rt>long-leh</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>乱撺起</rb><rt>lön-tsʽön-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>下手</rb><rt>ʽô-siu</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>响响</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>帮助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>各到处</rb><rt>kôh-tao-cʽü</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt><rb>抵敌</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>*ah-lah*</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>领勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>污秽</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> （<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>特罗非摩</rb><rt>Deh-lo-fi-mo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>道是</rb><rt>dao-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>进</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>去过了</rb><rt>kyʽi-ko-de</rt></ruby>。）<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>满</rb><rt>Mun</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>摇动</rb><rt>yiao-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>奔拢来</rb><rt>peng-long-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>抲勒</rb><rt>kʽô-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>拖出</rb><rt>tʽo-cʽih</rt><rb>圣殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>关了</rb><rt>kwæn-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>正</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>打账</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>行情</rb><rt>ʽông-dzing</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>营里个</rb><rt>ying-li-go</rt><rb>千总</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽEn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>吵乱</rb><rt>tsʽao-lön</rt></ruby>。」<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百总</rb><rt>pah-tsong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奔落</rb><rt>peng-lôh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>千总</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>营兵</rb><rt>ying-ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歇手</rb><rt>hyih-siu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>千总</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>两根</rb><rt>liang-keng</rt><rb>链条</rb><rt>lin-diao</rt><rb>锁住</rb><rt>so-djü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>*keh*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做过</rb><rt>tso-ko</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>怀怀响</rb><rt>wæ-wæ-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>纷纷不一</rb><rt>feng-feng-peh-ih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>千总</rb><rt>*tsʽin-tsong*</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>吵乱</rb><rt>tsʽao-lön</rt><rb>靠</rb><rt>kʽao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗实</rb><rt>feh-jih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>营里</rb><rt>ying-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>格步阶</rb><rt>kah-bu-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>拥挤</rb><rt>üong-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>营兵</rb><rt>ying-ping</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>揹</rb><rt>pe</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>Keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>怀怀响</rb><rt>wæ-wæ-hyiang</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>Kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>！」

<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>带进</rb><rt>ta-tsing</rt><rb>营里</rb><rt>ying-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>千总</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>千总</rb><rt>Tsʽin-tsong</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>？<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>作乱</rb><rt>tsôh-lön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>率领</rb><rt>seh-ling</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>四千</rb><rt>s-tsʽin</rt><rb>强盗</rb><rt>gyiang-dao</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基利家</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-li-kyüô-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大数</rb><rt>Da-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>城里个</rb><rt>zing-li-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>两句</rb><rt>*liang-kyü*</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] Keh Yiæ-gyih nying z Y. ʽE. 55 keh nyin tsôh-lön-go. 箇<u>埃及</u>人是耶（稣）后（头）55（年）箇年作乱个。（原注）</sup>

<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>千总</rb><rt>Tsʽin-tsong</rt><rb>应许了</rb><rt>ing-hyü-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>格步阶里</rb><rt>kah-bu-kæn-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>抈抈</rb><rt>yüih-yüih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*Gyi-lah*</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>静落了</rb><rt>zing-lôh-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>*Pao-lo*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>希伯来</u>个</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ-go</rt><rb>土音</rb><rt>tʽu-ing</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

---

### Sd. XXII.

「<ruby><rb>众位</rb><rt>Cong-we</rt><rb>叔伯</rb><rt>shoh-pah</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>分剖个</rb><rt>feng-pʽeo-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> （<ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>希伯来</u>个</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ-go</rt><rb>土音</rb><rt>tʽu-ing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>静办</rb><rt>zing-bæn</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>*Pao-lo*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：）<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基利家</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-li-kyüô-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大数</rb><rt>Da-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>养大</rb><rt>iang-do</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>迦玛列</u>个</rb><rt>Kyüô-mô-lih-go</rt><rb>门下</rb><rt>meng-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>详细</rb><rt>dziang-si</rt><rb>学习</rb><rt>ʽôh-jih</rt><rb>祖宗个</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>热心</rb><rt>nyih-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>众位</rb><rt>cong-we</rt><rb>一样个</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>逼难</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt><rb>箇路</rb><rt>keh-lu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>地步</rb><rt>di-bu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>男女</rb><rt>nen-nyü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>缚仔</rb><rt>bo-ts</rt><rb>挈落</rb><rt>kyʽih-lôh</rt><rb>监里</rb><rt>kæn-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <sup>6</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大马色</rb><rt>Da-mô-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>约来</rb><rt>iah-læ</rt><rb>昼过</rb><rt>tsiu-ko</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>忽然</rb><rt>hweh-jün</rt><rb>天里</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>团圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>照着</rb><rt>tsiao-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt><rb>地垟里</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>声音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<u><ruby><rb>扫罗</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>扫罗</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>逼难</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？』<sup>8</sup> （<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>同队个</rb><rt>dong-de-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>吃𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>听弗出</rb><rt>tʽing-feh-cʽih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>声音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt></ruby>。）<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>？』<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大马色</rb><rt>Da-mô-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>定规</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道其</rb><rt>dao-gyi</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>11</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>亮光个</rb><rt>liang-kwông-go</rt><rb>照耀</rb><rt>tsiao-yiao</rt><rb>看弗见</rb><rt>kʽen-feh-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>𠡒勒</rb><rt>te-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大马色</rb><rt>Da-mô-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>滰掉</rb><rt>gyiang-diao</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>17</sup> 「<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>*ʽEo-deo*</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>走转</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>倦去</rb><rt>gyün-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>接受个</rb><rt>tsih-ziu-go</rt></ruby>。』<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>挈</rb><rt>kyʽih</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>牢监</rb><rt>lao-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>聚会堂里</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>打账</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>远远</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>别国</rb><rt>bih-koh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>*Cong-nying*</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>声音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>怀起</rb><rt>wæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>Keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>除</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>掉</rb><rt>diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>活东</rb><rt>weh-tong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>胡咙响亮</rb><rt>wu-long-hyiang-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>脱掉</rb><rt>tʽeh-diao</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>灰尘</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt><rb>朝</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>乱</rb><rt>lön</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>千总</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>进</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>营里</rb><rt>ying-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>鞭子</rb><rt>pin-ts</rt><rb>拷打</rb><rt>kʽao-tang</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>*cong-nying*</rt><rb>箇种介</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>怀</rb><rt>wæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>皮带</rb><rt>bi-ta</rt><rb>绑</rb><rt>pông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>立东个</rb><rt>lih-tong-go</rt><rb>百总</rb><rt>pah-tsong</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>定罪</rb><rt>ding-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>百总</rb><rt>Pah-tsong</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>千总</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小心</rb><rt>siao-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>千总</rb><rt>Tsʽin-tsong</rt><rb>走拢去</rb><rt>tseo-long-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是个</rb><rt>Z-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>千总</rb><rt>Tsʽin-tsong</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>买个</rb><rt>ma-go</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>就是个</rb><rt>*ziu-z-go*</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拷打</rb><rt>kʽao-tang</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走开</rb><rt>tseo-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>千总</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>缚勒</rb><rt>bo-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>吃𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>DI-NYI</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>确确实实</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kyʽüoh-jih-jih</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>缚</rb><rt>bo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>全</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落来</rb><rt>lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

---

### Sd. XXIII.

<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>清盯</rb><rt>tsʽing-ting</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>众位</rb><rt>Cong-we</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>依勒</rb><rt>i-leh</rt><rb>良心</rb><rt>liang-sing</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>Tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚拿尼亚</rb><rt>Üô-nô-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>旁边</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt><rb>立东个</rb><rt>lih-tong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>白粉</rb><rt>bah-feng</rt><rb>刷出个</rb><rt>shih-cʽih-go</rt><rb>墙</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>坐仔</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>审</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>违背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>旁边</rb><rt>Bông-pin</rt><rb>立东个</rb><rt>lih-tong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>𧮙</rb><rt>zôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>坏</rb><rt>w̆a</rt></ruby>。』 」

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>其中</rb><rt>*gyi-cong*</rt><rb>有得</rb><rt>yiu-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒土该</rb><rt>Sah-tʽu-kæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有得</rb><rt>yiu-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>讴响</rb><rt>eo-hyiang</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>众位</rb><rt>Cong-we</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>判断了</rb><rt>pʽun-tön-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒土该</rb><rt>Sah-tʽu-kæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>争起来</rb><rt>tsang-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>两下</rb><rt>liang-ʽô</rt><rb>分开</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒土该</rb><rt>Sah-tʽu-kæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>再活个</rb><rt>tsæ-weh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>样</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>胡咙响亮</rb><rt>wu-long-hyiang-liang</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>争论</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>寻弗出</rb><rt>zing-feh-cʽih</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>坏处</rb><rt>w̆a-cʽü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>抵敌</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>既经</rb><rt>Kyi-kying</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>争造</rb><rt>tsang-zao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>千总</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>𤖼吃</rb><rt>pʽah-kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>夺</rb><rt>deh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>带进</rb><rt>ta-tsing</rt><rb>营里</rb><rt>ying-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>当夜</rb><rt>Tông-yia</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>放心</rb><rt>fông-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>切身</rb><rt>tsʽih-sing</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>TAO-LEH</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>结了</rb><rt>kyih-leh</rt><rb>党</rb><rt>tông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>罚</rb><rt>Vah</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>咒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四十多</rb><rt>s-jih-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>罚</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>甚大咒</rb><rt>zing-do-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>烦</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>千总</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落来</rb><rt>lôh-læ</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>详细</rb><rt>dziang-si</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将等</rb><rt>tsiang-teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>准备</rb><rt>cing-be</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>姊妹个</rb><rt>tsi-me-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>弶</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>营里</rb><rt>ying-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>百总</rb><rt>pah-tsong</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>后生</rb><rt>ʽeo-sang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>千总</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>千总</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>犯人</rb><rt>Væn-nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>后生</rb><rt>ʽeo-sang</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>千总</rb><rt>Tsʽin-tsong</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拉勒</rb><rt>l̆a-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>？」<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>约好</rb><rt>iah-hao</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>公会里</rb><rt>kong-we-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>察访</rb><rt>tsʽah-fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>详细</rb><rt>dziang-si</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>听信</rb><rt>tʽing-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>内中</rb><rt>nen-cong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四十多</rb><rt>s-jih-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弶</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>间</rb><rt>kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>准备</rb><rt>cing-be</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>单等</rb><rt>tæn-teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>千总</rb><rt>Tsʽin-tsong</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>后生</rb><rt>ʽeo-sang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>箇起</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>千总</rb><rt>*Tsʽin-tsong*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>百总</rb><rt>pah-tsong</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>*teng*</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>备办</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>二百</rb><rt>nyi-pah</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>马兵</rb><rt>mô-ping</rt><rb>七十</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>长枪手</rb><rt>dziang-tsʽiang-siu</rt><rb>二百</rb><rt>nyi-pah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连夜</rb><rt>lin-yia</rt><rb>二更</rb><rt>nyi-kang</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒利亚</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>备办</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>骑仔</rb><rt>gyi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>护送</rb><rt>wu-song</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>总督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓力士</rb><rt>Fi-lih-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>一封</rb><rt>ih-fong</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>下文</rb><rt>ʽô-veng</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>：

<sup>26</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>革老丢</rb><rt>Keh-lao-tiu</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>吕西亚</rb><rt>Li-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>写</rb><rt>*sia*</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>*sing*</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>总督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓力士</rb><rt>Fi-lih-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大人</rb><rt>da-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>请请</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽing</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>抲去</rb><rt>kʽô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>公会里</rb><rt>kong-we-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt><rb>不过</rb><rt>*peh-ko*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>关着</rb><rt>kwæn-djôh</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>缚</rb><rt>bo</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>罪名</rb><rt>ze-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>*ʽEo-deo*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>打账</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>弶</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>计较</rb><rt>kyi-kao</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>随手</rb><rt>ze-siu</rt><rb>送上来</rb><rt>song-zông-læ</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>原告</rb><rt>nyün-kao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>康健</rb><rt>kʽông-gyin</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>*gyi-go*</rt><rb>号令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>连夜</rb><rt>lin-yia</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提帕底</rb><rt>En-di-pʽô-ti</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>马兵</rb><rt>mô-ping</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>营里</rb><rt>ying-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒利亚</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇封</rb><rt>keh-fong</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>送上</rb><rt>song-zông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>总督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>总督</rb><rt>Tsong-toh</rt><rb>看好</rb><rt>kʽen-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>省</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基利家</rb><rt>Kyi-li-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>原告</rb><rt>nyün-kao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>详细</rb><rt>dziang-si</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>希律</u>个</rb><rt>Hyi-lih-go</rt><rb>王宫里</rb><rt>wông-kong-li</rt><rb>押</rb><rt>ah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>间</rb><rt>kæn</rt></ruby>。

---

### Sd. XXIV.

<ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>KO-LEH</rt><rb>五</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚拿尼亚</rb><rt>Üô-nô-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>会讲会话个</rb><rt>we-kông-we-wô-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>帖土罗</rb><rt>Tʽiah-tʽu-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>落来</rb><rt>lôh-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>总督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讴来</rb><rt>eo-læ</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>帖土罗</rb><rt>Tʽiah-tʽu-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>太太平平</rb><rt>tʽa-tʽa-bing-bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>亏了</rb><rt>kyʽü-leh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>先见之明</rb><rt>sin-kyin-ts-ming</rt><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt><rb>光明正大</rb><rt>kwông-ming-tsing-da</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一处</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>*sô-yi*</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>各到处</rb><rt>kôh-tao-cʽü</rt><rb>时常</rb><rt>z-djông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>感恩个</rb><rt>ken-eng-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>谢谢</rb><rt>zia-zia</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>腓力士</rb><rt>Fi-lih-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大人</rb><rt>da-jing</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>烦</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>宽恕</rb><rt>kwʽun-shü</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>领径</rb><rt>ling-kying</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>*kông*</rt><rb>两句</rb><rt>*liang-kyü*</rt></ruby>。
