<sup>5</sup> 「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>瘟人</rb><rt>w̆eng-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吵乱</rb><rt>tsʽao-lön</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>教个</rb><rt>kyiao-go</rt><rb>头脑</rb><rt>deo-nao</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>犯着</rb><rt>væn-djôh</rt><rb>圣殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打账</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>千总</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>吕西亚</rb><rt>Li-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>强</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>夺了去</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>原告</rb><rt>nyün-kao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>*Næn-kæn*</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>随勒</rb><rt>ze-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>事干</rb><rt>Z-ken</rt><rb>实在</rb><rt>*jih-dzæ*</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>总督</rb><rt>Tsong-toh</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>𩑦𩑦</rb><rt>w̆eh-w̆eh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>国个</rb><rt>koh-go</rt><rb>判断官</rb><rt>pʽun-tön-kwun</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>放心</rb><rt>fông-sing</rt><rb>分剖</rb><rt>feng-pʽeo</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>礼拜</rb><rt>li-pa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讲讲</rb><rt>kông-kông</rt><rb>只勒</rb><rt>tsih-leh</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>并</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>辩论</rb><rt>bin-leng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>吵乱</rb><rt>tsʽao-lön</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>聚会堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>各到处</rb><rt>kôh-tao-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>*kʽen-kyin*</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*ngô*</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>还弗出</rb><rt>wæn-feh-cʽih</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>*Tsih-yiu*</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>招认</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>邪</rb><rt>*zia*</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>祖宗个</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>先知人个</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>*shü-li*</rt><rb>写的个</rb><rt>sia-tih-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>想望个</rb><rt>siang-vông-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我自</rb><rt>ngô-zi</rt><rb>勉励</rb><rt>min-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>存</rb><rt>dzeng</rt><rb>对勒住个</rb><rt>te-leh-djü-go</rt><rb>良心</rb><rt>liang-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Fi-lih-z* *tso* *Yiu-tʽa-go* *tsong-toh*, *z* *Y*. *ʽE*. *53* *zông-dzing*. <u>腓力士</u>做<u>犹太</u>个总督，是耶（稣）后（头）53（年）上任。（原注）</sup>

<sup>17</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>*li-kʽæ*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>*Yiu-tʽa*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>几</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>归来</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>周济</rb><rt>tsiu-tsi</rt><rb>我自</rb><rt>ngô-zi</rt><rb>本国人</rb><rt>peng-koh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>*hyin*</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚西亚</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>闹热</rb><rt>nao-nyih</rt></ruby>，<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>批评</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*ngô*</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>来东个</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>错处</rb><rt>tsʽo-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>话出来</rb><rt>wô-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讴响个</rb><rt>eo-hyiang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>『<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt><rb>再活个</rb><rt>tsæ-weh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>判断了</rb><rt>pʽun-tön-de</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>除勒</rb><rt>djü-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>*m-kao*</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>*soh-go*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*ngô*</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>腓力士</rb><rt>Fi-lih-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>道理个</rb><rt>dao-li-go</rt><rb>详细</rb><rt>dziang-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>宽约</rb><rt>kwʽun-iah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>千总</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>吕西亚</rb><rt>Li-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落来</rb><rt>lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>审结</rb><rt>sing-kyih</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>百总</rb><rt>pah-tsong</rt><rb>管押</rb><rt>kwun-ah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>宽待</rb><rt>kwʽun-dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>供应</rb><rt>kong-ing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>甮</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>阻住</rb><rt>tsu-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>几</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>腓力士</rb><rt>Fi-lih-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>土西拉</rb><rt>Tʽu-si-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>犹太</u>个</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>节制个</rb><rt>tsih-cü-go</rt><rb>行为</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>判断个</rb><rt>pʽun-tön-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>腓力士</rb><rt>Fi-lih-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吃𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>*teng*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>便个</rb><rt>bin-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>给</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>屡次</rb><rt>le-tsʽ</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<u><ruby><rb>波求</rb><rt>Po-gyiu</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>非士都</rb><rt>Fi-z-tu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>接</rb><rt>tsih</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>腓力士</u>个</rb><rt>Fi-lih-z-go</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>腓力士</rb><rt>Fi-lih-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>凑</rb><rt>tsʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>趣</rb><rt>cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>给</rb><rt>coh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>关东</rb><rt>kwæn-tong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 62. 耶（稣）后（头）62（年）。（原注）</sup>

---

### Sd. XXV.

<u><ruby><rb>非士都</rb><rt>FI-Z-TU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上任</rb><rt>zông-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-leh</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒利亚</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>Tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>犹太</u>个</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa-go</rt><rb>乡绅</rb><rt>hyiang-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>状</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>恩</rb><rt>eng</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>*zi*</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>路里</rb><rt>lu-li</rt><rb>弶</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>东</rb><rt>tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>斗勒</rb><rt>*teo-leh*</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>非士都</rb><rt>Fi-z-tu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒利亚</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>管押间</rb><rt>kwun-ah-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>庉弗得</rb><rt>deng-feh-teh</rt><rb>几</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>*yi*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>绅衿</rb><rt>sing-kying</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>落去</rb><rt>lôh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>坏处</rb><rt>*w̆a-cʽü*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>耽搁</rb><rt>tæn-kôh</rt><rb>十头八</rb><rt>jih-deo-pah</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒利亚</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>坐堂</rb><rt>zo-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>带到了</rb><rt>Ta-tao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>团圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>立东</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>还弗出</rb><rt>wæn-feh-cʽih</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>重头</rb><rt>djong-deo</rt><rb>罪名</rb><rt>ze-ming</rt><rb>攻击</rb><rt>kong-kyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>分剖</rb><rt>feng-pʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>圣殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>犯着</rb><rt>væn-djôh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Yiu-teh* *kwu-kyün* wô, jih-to nyih. 有得古卷话，十多日。（原注）</sup>

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>非士都</rb><rt>Fi-z-tu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>凑</rb><rt>tsʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>趣</rb><rt>cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>审</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>官司</rb><rt>kwun-s</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>该撒</u>个</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-go</rt><rb>法堂上</rb><rt>fah-dông-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>审判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>坏处</rb><rt>w̆a-cʽü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>明明</rb><rt>ming-ming</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>坏处</rb><rt>w̆a-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>犯着</rb><rt>væn-djôh</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>*yia*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>推辞</rb><rt>tʽe-dz</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>虚个</rb><rt>hyü-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>伸冤</rb><rt>sing-ün</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>非士都</rb><rt>Fi-z-tu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>商量仔</rb><rt>siang-liang-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>伸冤</rb><rt>sing-ün</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>*ka-ni*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u>。」

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>KO-LEH</rt><rb>几</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亚基帕</rb><rt>Üô-kyi-pʽô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>百尼基</rb><rt>Pah-nyi-kyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒利亚</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>非士都</rb><rt>Fi-z-tu</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇头</rb><rt>Keh-deo</rt><rb>庉勒</rb><rt>deng-leh</rt><rb>好几</rb><rt>hao-kyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>非士都</rb><rt>Fi-z-tu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>保罗</u>个</rb><rt>Pao-lo-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>告诉</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>荡头</rb><rt>*Dông-deo*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>腓力士</rb><rt>Fi-lih-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去个</rb><rt>*kyʽi-go*</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>*z-ʽeo*</rt><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>给</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>关东</rb><rt>kwæn-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*ngô*</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>被告</rb><rt>Be-kao</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>原告</rb><rt>nyün-kao</rt><rb>斗头</rb><rt>teo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>*feh-zing*</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>机会</rb><rt>kyi-we</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>分剖</rb><rt>feng-pʽeo</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>规矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt></ruby>。』<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>迟缓</rb><rt>dzi-wun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>坐堂</rb><rt>zo-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>原告</rb><rt>nyün-kao</rt><rb>立出来</rb><rt>lih-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>忖到个</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tao-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>议论</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>教门个</rb><rt>kyiao-meng-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>死过了</rb><rt>si-ko-liao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活个</rb><rt>weh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>断</rb><rt>tön</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>审</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>官司</rb><rt>kwun-s</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>御状</rb><rt>nyü-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*ngô*</rt><rb>押</rb><rt>ah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>东</rb><rt>tong</rt><rb>留</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西巴士督</rb><rt>Si-pô-z-toh</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>审判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>管押</rb><rt>kwun-ah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u>。」

<sup>\[1\] Si-pô-z-toh, *ʽôh* *wô* Üô-kwu-z-toh, *z* *Lo-mô* *wông-ti-go* *koh-ʽao*: Kæ-sah *yia* *z-ka*. <u>西巴士督</u>，或话<u>亚古士督</u>，是<u>罗马</u>人皇帝个国号。<u>该撒</u>也是介。（原注）</sup>

<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚基帕</rb><rt>Üô-kyi-pʽô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>非士都</rb><rt>Fi-z-tu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>非士都</rb><rt>Fi-z-tu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>明朝</rb><rt>Ming-tsiao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>DI-NYI</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚基帕</rb><rt>Üô-kyi-pʽô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>百尼基</rb><rt>Pah-nyi-kyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>摆勒</rb><rt>pa-leh</rt><rb>全副</rb><rt>djün-fu</rt><rb>执事</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>千总</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>城里个</rb><rt>zing-li-go</rt><rb>乡绅</rb><rt>hyiang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>公堂里</rb><rt>kong-dông-li</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>非士都</rb><rt>Fi-z-tu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>带出东</rb><rt>ta-cʽih-tong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>非士都</rb><rt>Fi-z-tu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>亚基帕</rb><rt>Üô-kyi-pʽô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东个</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt><rb>众位</rb><rt>cong-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>响响</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>犯过</rb><rt>væn-ko</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再话</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西巴士督</rb><rt>Si-pô-z-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>伸冤</rb><rt>sing-ün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>定见</rb><rt>ding-kyin</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>实情</rb><rt>jih-dzing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>奏明</rb><rt>tseo-ming</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*ngô*</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>众位</rb><rt>*cong-we*</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>要紧个</rb><rt>iao-kying-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚基帕</rb><rt>Üô-kyi-pʽô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王个</rb><rt>wông-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>查察</rb><rt>dzô-tsʽah</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>案卷</rb><rt>en-kyün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>于</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>理</rb><rt>li</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>合个</rb><rt>ʽeh-go</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Sd. XXVI.

<u><ruby><rb>亚基帕</rb><rt>Üô-kyi-pʽô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>准</rb><rt>Cing</rt><rb>尔自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt><rb>分剖</rb><rt>feng-pʽeo</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>提带起</rb><rt>di-tæn-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分剖</rb><rt>feng-pʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>2</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>亚基帕</rb><rt>Üô-kyi-pʽô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>分剖</rb><rt>feng-pʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>造化</rb><rt>zao-hwô</rt></ruby>，<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>拢总个</rb><rt>long-tsong-go</rt><rb>规矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>议论</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>耐心</rb><rt>næ-sing</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>本国人</rb><rt>peng-koh-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>严个</rb><rt>nyin-go</rt><rb>教门</rb><rt>kyiao-meng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>立仔</rb><rt>lih-ts</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>十二个</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日夜</rb><rt>nyih-yia</rt><rb>勤力</rb><rt>gying-lih</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>亚基帕</rb><rt>Üô-kyi-pʽô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>告了</rb><rt>kao-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> 「<ruby><rb>咋个</rb><rt>Dza-go</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>相信弗来</rb><rt>siang-sing-feh-læ</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>我自</rb><rt>Ngô-zi</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>方法</rb><rt>*fông-fah*</rt><rb>攻击</rb><rt>kong-kyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>祭司头个</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>接受勒</rb><rt>tsih-ziu-leh</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>圣徒</rb><rt>sing-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>关</rb><rt>kwæn</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>牢监</rb><rt>lao-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>得意</rb><rt>teh-i</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>聚会堂里</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>屡次</rb><rt>le-tsʽ</rt><rb>刑罚</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>硬抲</rb><rt>ngang-kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>亵渎</rb><rt>sih-doh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>切齿痛恨</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsʽ-tʽong-ʽeng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>外国</rb><rt>nga-koh</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>赶勒</rb><rt>ken-leh</rt><rb>逼难</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>托勒</rb><rt>tʽôh-leh</rt><rb>祭司头个</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo-go</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>号令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大马色</rb><rt>Da-mô-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>哦</rb><rt>ô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>昼过</rb><rt>tsiu-ko</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>天里个</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>日头</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>亮</rb><rt>liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>团圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>照着</rb><rt>tsiao-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>同路个</rb><rt>dong-lu-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt><rb>地垟里</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>希伯来</u>个</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ-go</rt><rb>土音</rb><rt>tʽu-ing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<u><ruby><rb>扫罗</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>扫罗</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>逼难</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>踢着</rb><rt>tʽih-djôh</rt><rb>刺</rb><rt>tsʽ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>难个</rb><rt>næn-go</rt></ruby>。』<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？』<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>逼难个</rb><rt>pih-næn-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>爬起</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>立东</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>特为</rb><rt>deh-we</rt><rb>现出来</rb><rt>yin-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>设立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>看见个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>现出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>*Yi-seh-lih*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>别国</rb><rt>bih-koh</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>眼</rb><rt>ngæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>*li-kʽæ*</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>heh-en</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>厉害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>撒但</u>个</rb><rt>Sah-dæn-go</rt><rb>势道</rb><rt>shü-dao</rt><rb>归服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>罪个</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>饶赦</rb><rt>nyiao-sô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尊起了</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi-liao</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>*teh-djôh*</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>19</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>亚基帕</rb><rt>Üô-kyi-pʽô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依顺</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>天里个</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>显灵</rb><rt>hyin-ling</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大马色</rb><rt>Da-mô-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶路撒冷</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>统</rb><rt>tʽong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>*ʽeo-deo*</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt><rb>归服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>行为</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>动手</rb><rt>dong-siu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*ngô*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> 「<ruby><rb>亏勒</rb><rt>Kyʽü-leh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>保护</rb><rt>pao-wu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>立牢东</rb><rt>lih-lao-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>大大小小</rb><rt>do-do-siao-siao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>别样</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>*kông*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>难</rb><rt>næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>头一个</rb><rt>deo-ih-go</rt><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>*Yi-seh-lih*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>正</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>分剖</rb><rt>feng-pʽeo</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>非士都</rb><rt>Fi-z-tu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>怀</rb><rt>wæ</rt><rb>一声</rb><rt>ih-sing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>发狂的了</rb><rt>fah-gwông-tih-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>文理</rb><rt>veng-li</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>弄勒</rb><rt>long-leh</rt><rb>发狂了</rb><rt>fah-gwông-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>非士都</rb><rt>Fi-z-tu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大人</rb><rt>da-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>发狂</rb><rt>fah-gwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>实实惠惠</rb><rt>jih-jih-we-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>忖当个</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tông-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>Keh-we</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>放胆</rb><rt>fông-tæn</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>壁</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>角落头</rb><rt>kôh-lôh-den</rt><rb>幽勒</rb><rt>iu-leh</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚基帕</rb><rt>Üô-kyi-pʽô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚基帕</rb><rt>Üô-kyi-pʽô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt><rb>省力</rb><rt>sang-lih</rt><rb>劝醒</rb><rt>kyʽün-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>徒</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-du</rt></ruby>！」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>省力</rb><rt>sang-lih</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>省力</rb><rt>sang-lih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>各样</rb><rt>kôh-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>除出</rb><rt>djü-cʽih</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>链条</rb><rt>lin-diao</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>箇番</rb><rt>Keh-fæn</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>讲好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>总督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>百尼基</rb><rt>Pah-nyi-kyi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>坐间个</rb><rt>zo-kæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>立起身</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>走勒</rb><rt>Tseo-leh</rt><rb>过点</rb><rt>ko-tin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>管押</rb><rt>kwun-ah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。」<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚基帕</rb><rt>Üô-kyi-pʽô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>非士都</rb><rt>Fi-z-tu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>伸冤</rb><rt>sing-ün</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Sd. XXVII.

<ruby><rb>既经</rb><rt>KYI-KYING</rt><rb>定规拨</rb><rt>ding-kwe-peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西巴士督</rb><rt>Si-pô-z-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>营里个</rb><rt>ying-li-go</rt><rb>百总</rb><rt>pah-tsong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹流</rb><rt>Yiu-liu</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚大米田</rb><rt>Üô-da-mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>驶过</rb><rt>s-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚西亚</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>开出去</rb><rt>kʽæ-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>马其顿</u>个</rb><rt>Mô-gyi-teng-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>帖撒罗尼迦</rb><rt>Tʽiah-sah-lo-nyi-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚里达古</rb><rt>Üô-li-dah-kwu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>同队个</rb><rt>dong-de-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西顿</rb><rt>Si-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>抛</rb><rt>pʽao</rt><rb>锚</rb><rt>mao</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>犹流</rb><rt>Yiu-liu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>好好</rb><rt>hao-hao</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>*gyi-lah-go*</rt><rb>照应</rb><rt>tsiao-ing</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>居比路</rb><rt>Kyü-pi-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>下风岸</rb><rt>ʽô-fong-ngen</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>斗风个</rb><rt>teo-fong-go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>驶过勒</rb><rt>S-ko-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基利家</rb><rt>Kyi-li-kyüô</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>旁非利亚</rb><rt>Bông-fi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近个</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>吕家</u>个</rb><rt>Li-kyüô-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>每拉</rb><rt>Me-lah</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>百总</rb><rt>pah-tsong</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚力山太</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-lih-sæn-tʽa-go</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>驶</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>箇只</rb><rt>keh-tsah</rt><rb>船里</rb><rt>jün-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>船</rb><rt>Jün</rt><rb>慢慢</rb><rt>mæn-mæn</rt><rb>驶勒</rb><rt>s-leh</rt><rb>好几</rb><rt>hao-kyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>乞乞喀喀</rb><rt>kyʽih-kyʽih-kʽah-kʽah</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>革尼土</u>个</rb><rt>Keh-nyi-tʽu-go</rt><rb>对面</rb><rt>te-min</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>风</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>阻住</rb><rt>tsu-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒摩尼</rb><rt>Sah-mo-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>驶</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>革哩底</u>个</rb><rt>Keh-li-ti-go</rt><rb>下风岸</rb><rt>ʽô-fong-ngen</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>趁</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>岸</rb><rt>ngen</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>乞乞喀喀</rb><rt>kyʽih-kyʽih-kʽah-kʽah</rt><rb>驶</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>佳澳</rb><rt>Kyüô-ao</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉西亚</rb><rt>Lah-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>Z-ʽeo</rt><rb>长久</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>禁食个</rb><rt>kying-zih-go</rt><rb>节肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>*ah-lah*</rt><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>船路</rb><rt>jün-lu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>险险个</rb><rt>hyin-hyin-go</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>劝劝</rb><rt>kyʽün-kyʽün</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>10</sup> 「<ruby><rb>朋友</rb><rt>Beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇伐</rb><rt>keh-vah</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>苦头</rb><rt>kwʽu-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>损伤</rb><rt>seng-sông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>货物</rb><rt>ho-veh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>连累</rb><rt>lin-le</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>百总</rb><rt>pah-tsong</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>老大</rb><rt>lao-da</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其命</rb><rt>gyi-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>过如</rb><rt>ko-jü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>保罗</u>个</rb><rt>Pao-lo-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Kying-zih-go tsih-kʽeng, *z* *teng* *ʽEn-lu-tsih* *tso* *z-ʽeo*. 禁食个节肯，是等寒露节做时候。（原注）</sup>

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>岙</rb><rt>ao</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>冬</rb><rt>tong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>便</rb><rt>bin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大半</rb><rt>ih-do-pun</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>开出去</rb><rt>kʽæ-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓尼革</rb><rt>Fi-nyi-keh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>冬</rb><rt>tong</rt></ruby>。（<u><ruby><rb>腓尼革</rb><rt>*Fi-nyi-keh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革哩底</rb><rt>Keh-li-ti</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>岙</rb><rt>ao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一边</rb><rt>*ih-pin*</rt><rb>朝</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>西南</rb><rt>si-nen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一边</rb><rt>*ih-pin*</rt><rb>朝</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>西北</rb><rt>si-poh</rt></ruby>。）<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>Kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>南风</rb><rt>nen-fong</rt><rb>缓缓</rb><rt>wun-wun</rt><rb>扬起来</rb><rt>yiang-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>得意</rb><rt>teh-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>锚</rb><rt>*mao*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>趁</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革哩底</rb><rt>Keh-li-ti</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>岸</rb><rt>ngen</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>有限</rb><rt>yiu-ʽæn</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>狂风</rb><rt>gwông-fong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>友罗革腾</rb><rt>Yiu-lo-keh-deng</rt></ruby>」，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>岸上</rb><rt>ngen-zông</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>别落来</rb><rt>bih-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>船</rb><rt>Jün</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>风</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>吹勒去</rb><rt>cʽü-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吃弗住</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-feh-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>随</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>风</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>闯</rb><rt>cʽông</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>闯</rb><rt>Cʽông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>海岛个</rb><rt>hæ-tao-go</rt><rb>下风岸</rb><rt>ʽô-fong-ngen</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>海岛</rb><rt>*hæ-tao*</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革老底</rb><rt>Keh-lao-ti</rt></ruby></u>，）<ruby><rb>舢板</rb><rt>sæn-pæn</rt><rb>乞乞喀喀</rb><rt>kyʽih-kyʽih-kʽah-kʽah</rt><rb>收上</rb><rt>siu-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>收上仔</rb><rt>Siu-zông-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>绳索</rb><rt>zing-soh</rt><rb>捆勒</rb><rt>kwʽeng-leh</rt><rb>船底</rb><rt>jün-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>搁着</rb><rt>gôh-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>苏耳底</rb><rt>Su-r-ti</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>沙</rb><rt>sô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>放落</rb><rt>fông-lôh</rt><rb>大篷</rb><rt>do-bong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>闯勒去</rb><rt>cʽông-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>狂风</rb><rt>gwông-fong</rt><rb>丢来丢去</rb><rt>tiu-læ-tiu-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>送载</rb><rt>song-zæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>亲手</rb><rt>tsʽing-siu</rt><rb>丢掉</rb><rt>tiu-diao</rt><rb>船里个</rb><rt>jün-li-go</rt><rb>工具</rb><rt>kong-gyü</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>特买</rb><rt>Deh-ma</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>日头</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>星宿</rb><rt>sing-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>狂风</rb><rt>gwông-fong</rt><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>想头</rb><rt>siang-deo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>断绝了</rb><rt>dön-djih-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Yiu-teh* *fæn*, teo-deo. 有得翻，斗头。（原注）</sup>

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>*Cong-nying*</rt><rb>长久</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>立起身</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>朋友</rb><rt>Beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革哩底</rb><rt>Keh-li-ti</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djôh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>苦头</rb><rt>kwʽu-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>损伤</rb><rt>seng-sông</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>劝</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>放心</rb><rt>fông-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>djü-leh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>伤</rb><rt>sông</rt><rb>一条</rb><rt>ih-diao</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>归服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>供奉个</rb><rt>kong-vong-go</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>昨夜</rb><rt>zô-yia</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>旁边</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>24</sup> 『<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>甮</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>该撒</u>个</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>同船个</rb><rt>dong-jün-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。』<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>众位</rb><rt>cong-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>放心</rb><rt>fông-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>*z-ken*</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>*tʽin-s*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>搁</rb><rt>gôh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>海岛里</rb><rt>hæ-tao-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>第十四</rb><rt>di-jih-s</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>*jün*</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚底亚</rb><rt>Üô-ti-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>海里</rb><rt>*hæ-li*</rt><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>闯勒去</rb><rt>cʽông-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>半夜过</rb><rt>pun-yia-ko</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>水手</rb><rt>shü-siu</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>近</rb><rt>gying</rt><rb>岸</rb><rt>ngen</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>深浅</rb><rt>sing-tsʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>廿仞</rb><rt>nyiæn-jing</rt><rb>深</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>过去点</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi-tin</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十五仞</rb><rt>jih-ng-jing</rt><rb>深</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>砑礁</rb><rt>ngô-tsiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>后梢</rb><rt>ʽeo-sao</rt><rb>抛</rb><rt>pʽao</rt><rb>四门</rb><rt>s-meng</rt><rb>锚</rb><rt>mao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>单</rb><rt>tæn</rt><rb>忏念</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyiæn</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>水手</rb><rt>Shü-siu</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>舢板</rb><rt>sæn-pæn</rt><rb>放落</rb><rt>fông-lôh</rt><rb>海里</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>假戏头</rb><rt>kô-hyi-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>抛</rb><rt>pʽao</rt><rb>船头个</rb><rt>jün-deo-go</rt><rb>锚</rb><rt>mao</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百总</rb><rt>pah-tsong</rt><rb>营兵</rb><rt>ying-ping</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>船里</rb><rt>jün-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒救星了</rb><rt>m-kyiu-sing-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>营兵</rb><rt>Ying-ping</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>斩断</rb><rt>tsæn-dön</rt><rb>舢板个</rb><rt>sæn-pæn-go</rt><rb>绳索</rb><rt>zing-soh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>氽勒去</rb><rt>tʽeng-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>天</rb><rt>Tʽin</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>亮</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>劝</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>饭</rb><rt>væn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>十四</rb><rt>jih-s</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>风</rb><rt>*fong*</rt><rb>息</rb><rt>*sih*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>长通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>饿勒</rb><rt>ngo-leh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>劝</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>吃点</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-tin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>于</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相干</rb><rt>siang-ken</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>头里个</rb><rt>deo-li-go</rt><rb>头发</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt><rb>一根</rb><rt>ih-keng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>褪落</rb><rt>tʽeng-lôh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>讲好</rb><rt>Kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>谢谢</rb><rt>zia-zia</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>𤖼带开</rb><rt>pʽah-tæn-kʽæ</rt><rb>吃了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>放心</rb><rt>fông-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>吃了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>船里</rb><rt>jün-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>二百</rb><rt>nyi-pah</rt><rb>七十</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih</rt><rb>六个</rb><rt>loh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>Yi-kying</rt><rb>吃饱了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>麦</rb><rt>mah</rt><rb>丢勒</rb><rt>tiu-leh</rt><rb>海里</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>轻点</rb><rt>kyʽing-tin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>认得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>岙</rb><rt>ao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>沙涂</rb><rt>sô-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>搁</rb><rt>gôh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>悬掉</rb><rt>yün-diao</rt><rb>锚</rb><rt>mao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>候</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>沉勒</rb><rt>dzing-leh</rt><rb>海里</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放松</rb><rt>fông-song</rt><rb>舵里个</rb><rt>do-li-go</rt><rb>绳索</rb><rt>zing-soh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>扬起</rb><rt>yiang-kyʽi</rt><rb>头篷</rb><rt>deo-bong</rt><rb>使</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>风</rb><rt>fong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>岸上</rb><rt>ngen-zông</rt><rb>进去</rb><rt>tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>Pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>两埭</rb><rt>liang-da</rt><rb>潮水</rb><rt>dziao-shü</rt><rb>别流个</rb><rt>bih-liu-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>浅沙</rb><rt>tsʽin-sô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>船头</rb><rt>jün-deo</rt><rb>搁牢</rb><rt>gôh-lao</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>动</rb><rt>dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后艄</rb><rt>ʽeo-sao</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>浪</rb><rt>lông</rt><rb>健</rb><rt>gyin</rt><rb>打破了</rb><rt>tang-pʽo-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>营兵</rb><rt>Ying-ping</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>游过去</rb><rt>yiu-ko-kyʽi</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>百总</rb><rt>pah-tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>许</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>识水性个</rb><rt>sih-shü-sing-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>跳落</rb><rt>tʽiao-lôh</rt><rb>水里</rb><rt>*shü-li*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>岸上</rb><rt>ngen-zông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>余多</rb><rt>yü-to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>靠勒</rb><rt>kʽao-leh</rt><rb>板</rb><rt>pæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>靠勒</rb><rt>kʽao-leh</rt><rb>船里个</rb><rt>jün-li-go</rt><rb>别样</rb><rt>*bih-yiang*</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>逃上去</rb><rt>*dao-zông-kyʽi*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>救上</rb><rt>kyiu-zông</rt><rb>岸</rb><rt>ngen</rt></ruby>。

---

### Sd. SX VIII.

<ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>Kyi-jün</rt><rb>救上</rb><rt>kyiu-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>才方</rb><rt>dzæ-fông</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>海岛</rb><rt>hæ-tao</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米利大</rb><rt>Mi-li-da</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>蛮人</rb><rt>mæn-nying</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>落雨</rb><rt>lôh-yü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再话</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt><rb>天家</rb><rt>tʽin-kô</rt><rb>冷</rb><rt>lang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>生勒</rb><rt>sang-leh</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>收留</rb><rt>siu-liu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撮拢</rb><rt>tsʽeh-long</rt><rb>一摙</rb><rt>ih-lin</rt><rb>柴</rb><rt>za</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>加勒</rb><rt>kô-leh</rt><rb>火里</rb><rt>ho-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一根</rb><rt>ih-keng</rt><rb>毒蛇</rb><rt>doh-dzô</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>热</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>窜出来</rb><rt>tsʽön-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咬着</rb><rt>ngao-djôh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>蛮人</rb><rt>mæn-nying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>毒蛇</rb><rt>doh-dzô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>保罗</u>个</rb><rt>Pao-lo-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>宕东</rb><rt>dang-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>凶手</rb><rt>hyüong-siu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>虽然</rb><rt>se-jün</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>海里</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt><rb>救上</rb><rt>kyiu-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>许</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>活东</rb><rt>weh-tong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>甩掉</rb><rt>hwah-diao</rt><rb>毒蛇</rb><rt>doh-dzô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>火里</rb><rt>ho-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>伤</rb><rt>sông</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>单等</rb><rt>tæn-teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>肿起来</rb><rt>cong-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>一时里</rb><rt>ih-z-li</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等了</rb><rt>teng-leh</rt><rb>长久</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>毛病</rb><rt>mao-bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>转</rb><rt>cün</rt><rb>念头</rb><rt>*nyiæn-deo*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>海岛个</rb><rt>hæ-tao-go</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>部百流</rb><rt>Bu-pah-liu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>收留</rb><rt>siu-liu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好好</rb><rt>hao-hao</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>Kʽeo-kʽeo</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>部百流</u>个</rb><rt>Bu-pah-liu-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>疟疾</rb><rt>nyiah-dzih</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>痢疾</rb><rt>li-dzih</rt><rb>困的</rb><rt>kwʽeng-tih</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祷告仔</rb><rt>tao-kao-ts</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>海岛里</rb><rt>hæ-tao-li</rt><rb>余多</rb><rt>yü-to</rt><rb>有病个</rb><rt>yiu-bing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>医好</rb><rt>i-hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>动身个</rb><rt>dong-sing-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>应用个</rb><rt>ing-yüong-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>KO-LEH</rt><rb>三个</rb><rt>sæn-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>趁</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚力山太</rb><rt>Üô-lih-sæn-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>船号</rb><rt>jün-ʽao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby>『<u><ruby><rb>丢士</rb><rt>Tiu-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>双生</rb><rt>shông-sang</rt></ruby>』，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>海岛</rb><rt>hæ-tao</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>冬</rb><rt>tong</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>开出去</rb><rt>kʽæ-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>叙拉古</rb><rt>Jü-lah-kwu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>抛</rb><rt>pʽao</rt><rb>锚</rb><rt>mao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>庉上</rb><rt>deng-zông</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>掉抢</rb><rt>diao-tsʽiang</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>驶</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利基翁</rb><rt>Li-kyi-ong</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-leh</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt><rb>南风</rb><rt>nen-fong</rt><rb>扬起来</rb><rt>yiang-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>部丢利</rb><rt>Bu-tiu-li</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇头</rb><rt>Keh-deo</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉上</rb><rt>deng-zông</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>信息</rb><rt>sing-sih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚比乌市</rb><rt>Üô-pi-u-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>三馆</rb><rt>Sæn-kwun</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>谢谢</rb><rt>zia-zia</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>胆子</rb><rt>tæn-ts</rt><rb>壮了</rb><rt>công-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 63. 耶（稣）后（头）63（年）。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] 帆船遇逆风时调整帆的角度，借助风力前进</sup>
