<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>百总</rb><rt>pah-tsong</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>将军</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>管守</rb><rt>kwun-siu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>Ko-LEH</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>请拢</rb><rt>tsʽing-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>为头为脑</rb><rt>we-deo-we-nao</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>来了</rb><rt>Læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>众位</rb><rt>Cong-we</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>犯着</rb><rt>væn-djôh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>*ah-lah*</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祖宗个</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>规矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>审问仔</rb><rt>sing-meng-ts</rt><rb>打账</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>犯着</rb><rt>væn-djôh</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>歇</rb><rt>hyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗得弗</rb><rt>feh-teh-feh</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>伸冤</rb><rt>sing-ün</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>拢来</rb><rt>long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>当面</rb><rt>tông-min</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>链条</rb><rt>lin-diao</rt><rb>缚住</rb><rt>bo-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>想望个</rb><rt>siang-vông-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>口传</rb><rt>kʽeo-djün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>讲起</rb><rt>kông-kyʽi</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>坏处</rb><rt>w̆a-cʽü</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>尔自个</rb><rt>ng-zi-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>教门</rb><rt>kyiao-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到处</rb><rt>tao-cʽü</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>批评</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>约定了</rb><rt>iah-ding-leh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>下处</rb><rt>ʽô-cʽü</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>*Pao-lo*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>先知人个</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt><rb>详细</rb><rt>dziang-si</rt><rb>解说</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>*dao-li*</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>劝</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>*Gyi-go*</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>两下</rb><rt>Liang-ʽô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>合头</rb><rt>ʽeh-deo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>走散了</rb><rt>tseo-sæn-de</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以赛亚</rb><rt>Yi-sæ-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>实在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>，<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听闻其</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见其</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>硬了</rb><rt>ngang-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>懒得</rb><rt>læn-teh</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>闭的</rb><rt>pi-tih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>医好</rb><rt>i-hao</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>救个</rb><rt>kyiu-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>讲好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>争论</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>PAO-LO</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>长</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>租的个</rb><rt>tsu-tih-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>放胆</rb><rt>fông-tæn</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>神国个</rb><rt>Jing-koh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>禁住</rb><rt>kying-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 65. 耶（稣）后（头）65（年）。（原注）</sup>
